648. “iṉṟiya irāma - ilakkuvar vi.ji. paṉṉirtās, cantōṣam cakōtararkaḷ.”

649. “āṉmā-uyirē mutal. inta mutalip perukki vaḷarkkavē uṭal mutaliya aṉittum.”

650. “maṉitakulam eṉṟa orumi ātmā. inta ātmāvi aṉivarum vaḷappaṭuttavēṇṭum.”

651. “iḻivāṉatu eṉṟu karutappaṭupavikaḷi nāṭakaṅkaḷil kāṭṭakkūṭātu,”

652. “paṭittavarkaḷiṉ tavaṟukaḷālēyē varalāṟu kīḻmippaṭukiṟatu.”

653. “paṭittavarkaḷ paṭippiṟkēṟṟavāṟu tammuṭiya vāḻkkip pōkkukaḷi māṟṟik koḷḷātu pōṉāl paṭippiṉāl payaṉ illi.”

654. “taṉimaṉitaṉi uruvākkuvatu avaṉuṭiya vāḻkālattiṉ camutāya amippē.”

655. “muṉṉēṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟa pacittuṭippu illāta camutāyam evvaḷavu vacatikaḷ iruntālum muṉṉēṟātu.”

656. “muṉṉēṟṟattiṉ mutaṟpaṭi ārvat tuṭippē!”

657. “naṭiyil valakkāl - iṭakkāl otticintu naṭappatupōla, nirvāka amippu otticintu iyaṅkavēṇṭum.”

658. “ceyya muṭiyavilli” eṉṟu tiṭṭa miṭavē payappaṭukiṟavarkaḷ oṉṟumē ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ.”

659. “poruḷātāra muṉṉēṟṟam eṉpatu oru vaḻukkal maram. itil kavaṉamāka ēṟavēṇṭum.”

660. “vēliyic ceyyātavarkaḷ, vēli paṟipōvati virumpamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

661. “uṭalukkuc caluki taralām, uyirp pātukāppukku muraṇ illāmal! atupōla, camutāya nalaṉukku virōtamillāmal caluki kāṭṭa vēṇṭiya avaciyamiruntāl caluki kāṭṭalām.”

662. “ēḻmi tiṭīreṉa vantataṉṟu. palavita māṉa kēṭukaḷ mutirnta niliyil ēḻmi ākiṟatu.”

663. “palavitamāṉa uṇarvukaḷiṉ tokuppē nalvāḻkki.”

664. “ciṟiyavarkaḷ muraṭṭuttaṉattukkē kaṭṭuppaṭuvārkaḷ.”

665. “etirmaṟi iyakkaṅkaḷ ākkappaṇikaḷ ceyya iyalātu.”

666. “caṭṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa oṭṭi vaḻit tappittu ōṭiṉāl ōṭupavaṉik kuṟi collak kūṭātu. caṭṭattiyē kuṟi collavēṇṭum.”

667. “eppaṭiyō maṉitarkaḷ avarkaḷic cuṟṟiyē vaṭṭamiṭukiṟārkaḷ! maṟṟavarkaḷip paṟṟik.” kavalippaṭuvatākak kūṟuvatukūṭa taṉṉi vaḷarttuk koḷḷattāṉ!”

668. “vayiṟārac cāppiṭa virumpukiṟavarkaḷ uṭalāra uḻikka muṉvaravēṇṭum.”

669. “uḻikkāmal uṇpavarkaḷ, maṟṟavar kurutiyik kuṭippavarkaḷ.”

670. “kāṟṟu aṭikkum ticiyil maram cāyntāl maram viḻum. kāṟṟi etirttu niṉṟāl vāḻum. atupōl cūḻnilikaḷic cārntē vāḻkiṟavarkaḷ vīḻntuviṭuvārkaḷ. cūḻnilikaḷi etirttu māṟṟa muṉipavarkaḷē vāḻvārkaḷ.

671. “kavarcciyil mayakkupavavi naṉmi ceytal aritu.”

672. “kuṟikkōḷ taḻiiya vāḻkki iruntālē tiṭṭamiṭum maṉappōkku tōṉṟum.”

673. tiṭṭamiṭāta ceyalmuṟikaḷ muḻup payaṉit tarā.”

674. “celvattiṉ ūṟṟu nilamē!”

675. “piṟar paṇattukku, celavukkut tiṭṭa miṭupavarkaḷ eṇṇikki mikuti.”

676. “kāṟṟu vīcumpoḻutu tali teṟikkaccuṟṟum maraṅkaḷ palamaṟṟavi. atupōla cōtaṉikaḷ vantuṟṟa poḻutu alamarukiṟavarkaḷ palamaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

677. kaḻivukaḷumkūṭa, poruḷuṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷē!”

678. “taṉ muṉippuṭiyavarkaḷ kāriyaṅkaḷi viṭṭuviṭuvar.”

679. “uyir vāḻvil uḷḷa āciyē nōykaḷi vaḷarkkiṟatu; pātukākkiṟatu.”

680. “kāriyam oṉṟupōlat tōṉṟum. āṉāl, ataṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ oṉṟalla; palavēyām.”

681. “aḻukkāṟu nuṇmi vaṭivattil iruntālum āṟṟalik keṭukkum.”

682. “mūḷic cōmpal ceyaltiṟaṉik keṭukkum.”

683. “kālattiṉ viḷivukaḷiyellām aḷantu eṇṇi aṉupavippavarkaḷ kālatti aḷantu payaṉpaṭuttat tavaṟiviṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

684. “vaḷarcciyillāta araciyal kaṭciyic cārntavarkaḷ iṉṟu oḻukkak kēṭṭi-muṟikēṭāṉa atikārap pirayōkatti tēcīya mayappaṭutti viṭṭārkaḷ.”

685. “iṉṟiya araciyal kaṭcikaḷ-tērtal iyakkaṅkaḷē! araciyal iyakkaṅkaḷ alla!”

686. “iṉṟiya camutāya amippil uṭal valimimē ātikkam ceykiṟatu. aṟivaṟinta āḷumiyalla.”

687. “oru noṭip poḻutu vāḻkki niṉṟālum pala nāḷkaḷiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam taṭippaṭum.”

688. “virivu mikka iyantira katiyil ulakam pōyk koṇṭirukkiṟatu. āṉāl, intiyarkaḷ āmi vēkatti māṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷilli.”

689. “aracāṅkam vēlikaḷi uṟpatti ceyyum iyantiramalla. maṉitarkaḷētāṉ vēlikaḷi uruvākkik koḷḷavēṇṭum.”

690. “ācikāṭṭi vāṅkuvaṉa ellām oru vakiyāṉa curaṇṭalēyām.”

691. “veḷḷāṭu vēḷāṇmit tattuvam colvatu pōla, cila pattirikkikaḷip paṭitta aḷavilēyē cilar vimarcaṉap pulikaḷākiviṭu kiṉṟaṉar.”

692. “aṇikkappaṭum pāṅkip poṟuttē atikāram nilipeṟum; aṭippatil alla.”

693. “cukatti aṉupavikkum maṉappōkku vanta piṟaku uḻippāḷiyātal aritu.”

694. “vimarcaṉattiṟku veṭkappaṭupavarkaḷ vaḷarcciyi virumpātavarkaḷ.”

695. “vimarcaṉamum vaḷarcciyum kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷ.”

696. “iṉṟiya aṟivu nalkum karuvikaḷ aṉittum aṟivu eṉṟa peyaril aṟiyāmiyiyē vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉa.”

697. “nilattiṉi muḻutum payaṉpaṭuttum āṟṟalē iṉṉum maṉitaṉukku varavilli.”

698. “iyaṟkiyi vellum muyaṟciyil maṉitaṉ īṭupaṭavilli. iyaṟkiyi oṭṭittāṉ vaḷarntu varukiṉṟāṉ.”

699. “iṉṟu cātāraṇamāṉavi eṉṟu karutap peṟupavi aṉittum uyarntavikaḷēyām.”

700. “vitikaḷ - accamiṉṟi naṭakka utavuvaṉa. vitikaḷ - accamiṉṟik kāriyaṅkaḷ ceyya utavuvaṉa.”





701. “irāmaṉ taṉakkāka naṭantāṉ. kaṇṇaṉ maṟṟavarkaḷukkāka naṭantāṉ.”

702. “eṉakkut teriyum” eṉṟa muṭivē aṟiyāmiyiṉ mukaṭu.”

703. “oruvariṭam illāta oṉṟi, avaciyat tēvi karutip peṟṟāl kiyūṭṭu; kimāṟṟu; tēviyillāta oṉṟip peṟṟāl atu aṉpiṉ naṭimuṟi.”

704. “parāmarikkap peṟāta uṟavukaḷ paḻutuṟum.”

705. “pāñcālikku - kaṭiciyil aḻitta piṟaku, kaṇṇaṉ utavi ceytataṟkuk kāraṇam pāñcāliyiṉ taṉviṉi muyaṟci mutircciyaṭiyaṭṭum eṉpataṉālēyām.”

706. “taṉviṉi muyaṟci mutirvaṭiyātavarkaḷiṭam piṟaviṉi muyaṟcikaḷ tākkaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttip payaṉtarātu.”

707. “taṅkaḷ maṉivi, makkaḷ, cuṟṟam eṉṟu cuṟṟupavarkaḷ uyartarak kuṇamuṭiyavarāka iruttal iyalātu.”

708. “eṉṉa-etaṟkākac colliyiruppār eṉṟu āyvu ceyyāmalē oruvar mītu āttirap paṭupavarkaḷ akampāvikaḷ. utavi ceyvatik kaṭamiyāka vikkāmal kaṭṭuppāṭāka ākkuvārkaḷ.”

709. “payaṉuṭiyatāka oruvar ceyyum vēli amiyātupōṉāl, atu vipacārattiṟkuc camam.”

ta-6

710. “oḻuṅkuppaṭuttik koḷḷa maṟuppavarkaḷ oṉṟu tavaṟu ceypavarkaḷ. allatu cōmpēṟikaḷ.”

711. “enta oṉṟum tiruppit tarap peṟātu pōṉāl ulakil vaṟaṭci tōṉṟum.”

712. “paṇam varuvatum pōvatum pōtātu. ovvoru kācum varumpoḻutu naṟpaṇpukaḷuṭaṉ atāvatu aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉi-aṉpu mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ vantaṭiya vēṇṭum.”

713. “tamiḻiṉa orumippāṭu kāṇpatē cilampiṉ nōkkam.”

714. “perumi vantuṟumpoḻutu cilar aṭaṅkāmal akanti koḷvar. itu uṟavi, orumippāṭṭik keṭukkum.”

715. “vaḷḷuvam, cilampu mutaliyaṉa kūṟum uyarnta nōkkaṅkaḷic camutāyam muṟiyaṭittuviṭṭatu.”

716. “caṭṭaṅkaḷukkut tiruttaṅkaḷ-camutāyat .ētavikalaḷāka amiyavēṇṭum. itik kaṇkāṇittu araciṉ kavaṉattiṟkuk koṇṭuvara caṭṭa ālōcaṉi āṇikkuḻu tēvi.

717. “vāḻkkiyiṉ tuṇi nalamāka amiyum peṇ, aṉupavap poruḷākavum payaṉākavum irukkiṟāḷ.”

718. ñāṉam illātapeṇkaḷ uḷḷa camutāyam cīrkeṭum.”

719. “makkaḷit takutiyuṭiyavarkaḷāka vaḷarkkum vari nampikkiyum kaṇkāṇippum iṇintē irukkavēṇṭum.”

720. camutāya māṟṟaṅkaḷi, cīrtiruttaṅkaḷ mūlamē ceytuviṭa muṭiyātu. cīrtiruttam eṉpatu puṇ nāṟāmalirukkap puṇuku pūcuvatu pōlattāṉ; puṇ āṟātu.”

721. “intiya camūkam, nōy niṟinta camūkam. enta maruntālum nōy tīravē illi. puraṭciyē tēvi.”

722. “āti tirāviṭarkaḷukku eṉṟu taṉit tokuti amippu tavirkkappaṭṭatu. intiyāviṟku naṉmiyāyiṟṟu.

723. “kātal ceyyum vāyppu iruntālē camūkattil ellāt tīmikaḷum pōyviṭum.”

724. vakuppu vāri utavit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷa vari cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ vaḷarum.

725. “uttaravātam vaḻaṅkāta aracu uḷḷavari enta oru oḻukkamum makkaḷ maṉṟattil kāl koḷḷātu.”

726. paṭippataṉāl maṭṭumē naṟpaṇpukaḷ vantu viṭuvatilli. camūka nikaḻvukaḷ tām paṇpāṭṭukkuk kaḷaṉ.”

727. “vakuppu vāri utavit tiṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷa vari cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ vaḷarum.”

728. “camūkat takutikaḷē iṉṟuḷḷa piracciṉi. tīṇṭāmiyalla.”

729. “intiya camūkattiṉ aṭippaṭik kaṭṭumāṉattilēyē tīṇṭāmi, cāti vēṟṟumi mutaliya tīmikaḷ paṭintuḷḷaṉa. intat tīmikaḷi puraṭciyiṉ mūlamē māṟṟa iyalum.”

730. “naṭantu pōṉataṟkuk kavalip paṭṭup payaṉilli. iṉi naṭakka vēṇṭiyataṟkē kavalip paṭavēṇṭum”.

731. “piṟappiṉ cārpāl varum kuṇakkēṭukaḷi nīkkavē kalvi-kēḷvi”

732. “ētāvatu or uyar nōkkam vāḻkkiyi ākkiramikkātavari kuṟṟaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu viṭutali peṟa iyalātu.”

733. “vēli naṭakkum. āṉāl, naṭanta vēlikaḷik koṇṭu paṇi ceytār eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyakkūṭātu. avar vāḻum kālattiṉ tēvi āṟṟal-vāytta karuvikaḷ ākiya aṉittiyum kaṇakkil eṭuttuk koṇṭutāṉ aḷavi ceyya vēṇṭum.”

734. “ēḻikaḷāka vāḻum makkaḷi aṟivuṇarac ceyvatu kaṭumiyāṉa paṇi.”

735. “iyaṟkiyiṉ niyati vaḻi, koṇṭum koṭuttum vāḻum muṟiyē amintuḷḷatu. maraṅkaḷ maṇṇiliruntu taṇṇir, uram eṭuttuk koṇṭu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa. piṉ avi paḻutta ili kaḷi maṇṇukku uramākat tantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa”

736. koṭukkāmal eṭukkum vāḻkki curaṇṭal vāḻkki”

737. “vaṟumiyi aṉupavittup paḻakippōṉa vāḻkki”

738. “vāḻtal vēṟu vāḻkkiyiṉ nōkkam vēṟu. vāḻtalukkuriya muyaṟcikaḷ nōkkaṅkaḷic cārntaviyākā”

739. “oḻuṅku muṟiyillāta ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ payaṉit tarā.”

740. “aṟivārnta vāḻkkiyil piṉtaṅkiya nili, paṇpāṭṭuk kuṟivik kāṭṭum.”

741. “ācikaḷ niṟivēṟāmi, vāḻkkiyiṉ toṭakkaṅkaḷāka amiyum.”

742. “celavaḻikkap peṟāta kācukaḷum varavēyām.”

743. “payamum camūkat tīmiyē.”

744. “potumiyil uruvākum naṉmikku īṭu illi.

745. “perumpāṉmi uṇarviṉ vaḻippaṭṭa ātikkanili uṇarvunili ellāc camūkattiṉarukkum uṇṭu.”

746. “entap paṇikkum mutaṟpaṇi makkaḷip paṭippikkum paṇiyēyām!”

747. “paḻakiyavarkaḷ ellām naṇparkaḷ allar; paḻakātavarkaḷ ellām pakivarkaḷum allar.”

748. “uṭal nalattiṟkuc cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnili tēvi. atupōlavē uyir nalattiṟkum cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnili tēvi.”

749. “oruvarukkoruvar māṟik kavalip paṭṭālē putiya camutāyam tōṉṟiviṭum.”

750. “vāḻat teriyātavarkaḷukkuc ceyyappaṭum utavi pāḻukkiṟittatākum.”

751. “oruvariṉ iruppu uṇarappaṭum niliyē vāḻum nili.”

752. “uḻittup peṟāta celvam arumiyākak karutap peṟamāṭṭātu.”

753. cākkaṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ tēkkattik kāttirukkum kocu pōla, camūka nikaḻvukaḷil ēṟpaṭum tīmikaḷik kāttiruppar kayavar.”

754. “kūṭṭukkaṭi vittiruppavar tiṭīreṉṟu kūṭṭukkārarukkut teriyāmal taṉikkaṭi vippatu aiyattiṟku iṭamaḷikkum.

755. “nāttikattiṟku vaḷḷuvam āṇi niṟkātu.”

756. tām ceyyum paṇikaḷi matippīṭu ceyya maṟuppatu, paṇikaḷip payaṉuṟac ceyya virumpāmiyē yākum.”

757. “paṇikaḷ pakirvē nirvākam; pakirvukaḷ poṟuppuṭaṉ niṟivēṟṟappaṭātu pōṉāl noymmiyē tōṉṟum.”

758. “nāṭṭu makkaḷit toḻilāḷarkaḷākavē ākkavēṇṭum; kaṭaṉkārarkaḷākavum viyāpārikaḷākavum ākkakkūṭātu.”

759. “uṟpattiyoṭu toṭarpillāta etuvum puṇṇiyamāṉa kaṭamiyākātu.”

760. “nalla paṇi, viḷamparam illā malart tōṭṭam.”

761. “oḻuṅkuppaṭuttap paṭāta ceyalāl oṉpatu naṭakka vēṇṭiya iṭattil oṉṟutāṉ naṭakkum.”

762. “nitāṉamaṟṟavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ māṉatti, kīrttiyip paḻikkiṉṟaṉar.”

763. “enta oṉṟum tavirkka iyalātatu eṉṟu ākiviṭṭāl anta aḷavil aṭimittaṉam vantu viṭukiṟatu.”

764. “nallavarkaḷiyē meḷaṉam kavarccikkum, maṟṟavarkaḷi ārpparavamē kavarccikkum.”

765. “nākarikam cīrkulinta camutāyattil poymmiyē vāymi.”

766. “ulakiyal peyarāl anītikkum uṭaṉpaṭa vēṇṭi varukiṟatu.”

767. “pēy aracu ceytāl pēraṟamum kuṉṟu kiṟatu.”

768. “vāḻkkiyiṉ tēvikaḷ kuṟikkōḷ ākā. vāḻkkiyiṉ tēvikaḷiyum kaṭantatu kuṟikkōḷ.”

769. “maṉitaṉ paḻakka valiyiṉiṉṟu mīḷa maṟukkiṟāṉ.”

770. “piriviṉikaḷum oru vakiyil tīṇṭāmiyē.”

771. “paṇam koṭuppativiṭa, paṇatti campātippavaṉāka ākkutal uyariya paṇi.”

772. “matam makkaḷiṉ aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil aṉuṭṭāṉattil iruntāl eppōtum aḻiyātu.”

773. “nallavarkaḷukku irukkum āṟṟal, paṭikkum āṟṟal. keṭṭavarkaḷukku irukkum āṟṟal, aḻikkum āṟṟal.”

774. “naṉmiyic ceytāl maṭṭum pōtātu. yārukkākac ceyyappaṭukiṟatō avarkaḷ naṉmiyi aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum."

775. “ellārum pukaḻi-perumiyi virumpukiṟārkaḷ. atit tārāḷamāka maṟṟavarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkuka.”

776. “nallavaṇṇam makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkup paṇi ceyyum niṟuvaṉattiṟku ātaravu iruppa tilli.”

777. “arimā caṅkam, cuḻaṟkuḻuk kaḻakam pōṉṟa potu amippil iruppavarkaḷ, piriviṉikaḷi vaḷarttu uṟavik keṭukkum cātic caṅkaṅkaḷ, mata amippukaḷil paṅku peṟutal kūṭātu.”

778. “iṉṟu ellā vakiyāṉa paṇikaḷilum ciṟantatu, makkaḷiṭattil vāḻvu viruppaṅkaḷiyum urimikaḷiyum uṇarttutal.”

779. “viḷamparattālō, calukikaḷālō pēccālō vākkukaḷ vāṅkappaṭum vari makkaḷāṭci muṟi ciṟakkātu.”

780. “paṇattiṉ arumi aṟital vēṟu. paṇattiṉ mītu āci vittal vēṟu.”

781. “paṇattiṉ mītu viruppam koṇṭu, īṭṭi, muṟiyākap payaṉpaṭuttutalē nalvāḻkki.”

782. “ulaka orumi, pūta peḷatīkaṅkaḷāl ēṟpaṭṭirukkiṟatē tavira, āṉmīka rītiyāka ēṟpaṭa villi.”

783. “aṟiviyal ulakatti iṇittatu. uyirkkulatti iṇikkavilli.”

784. “matam iyakkamāka-vāḻkki muṟiyāka irunta variyil makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki niliyi uyarttap payaṉpaṭṭatu. matattiṟkut talivarkaḷum purōkitarkaḷum uruvāṉārkaḷē aṉṟiliruntu matam, makkaḷukku naṉmi ceyyavilli. tīmiyē ceytatu: ceytuvarukiṟatu.”

785. “maṟantum ciṟṟellikaḷil maṉitaṉ viruppam koḷḷakkūṭātu; viruppam kāṭṭakkūṭātu. itu vaḷarcciyi-āṉmāviṉ viṭutaliyi taṭukkum.”

786. “pollāta maṉitaṉ uruvākkiya ellikaḷiṉṟu viṭutali peṟutalē uṇmiyāṉa viṭutali.”

787. “namatu iru cevikaḷ oṉṟiyoṉṟu nōkkātu etirtticiyil amintiruppatiṉ nōkkamē iru pakkaṅkaḷiyum kēḷeṉpataṟkākavē.”

788. “aṟivārnta aṉpē, vāḻkkikkup payaṉpaṭum.”

789. “uṟpattik karuvikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāl maṉita vāḻnili uyarntatu.”

790. “pōrk karuvikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyāl maṉita nākarikam pāḻpaṭṭatu. maṉitakulam aḻiviṉ ellikkuc ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟatu.”

791. “nāṭu, moḻi, iṉam, matam ākiyaṉavaṟṟik kaṭantatu orumippāṭu.”

792. “orumippāṭu amiyāta nāṭṭil vaḷamāṉa vāḻkki amiyātu.”

793. “caṭṭattiṉ cila vitikaḷi amulpaṭuttum atikāratti atikāravarkkattiṟku vaḻaṅkakkūṭātu."

794. “cila caṭṭa vitikaḷi nīkkutal iyalātu. āṉāl, payaṉpaṭuttutal tavirkkappaṭutal vēṇṭum.”

795. “iṉṟiya araciyali, taṉṉala nayappum uṇarcciyumē iyakkukiṉṟaṉa.”

796. “intiyāvil tavaṟukaḷip poṟuttalla, etirppu, iyakkaṅkaḷ. pātikkappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ kūccal pōṭum tiṟamiyip poṟuttatu.”

797. “intiya mutalāḷikaḷ piriviṉi caktikaḷi vaḷarkkiṉṟaṉar, ēḻmiyiṉ tuṉpattāl tuyaruṟuvōṉ puraṭciyil īṭupaṭāmal irukka”

798. “intiyāvil kaṭcikaḷ vaḷarntuḷḷaṉa. araciyal vaḷara villi.”

799. “maṉitaṉi, maṉitaṉāka matippatu oḻukkam.”

800. “orumippāṭṭuṇarvu eṉpatu tavattiṉ tavam.”





801. “ciṉṉa ciṉṉa ceytikaḷil kāṭṭap peṟum akkaṟiyē periya periya cātaṉikaḷic ceyya utavi ceyyum.”

802. “atikāram uṭiyavarkaḷ naṉkoṭi kēṭṭāl kūṭa atu nirppantāṉ.”

803. “tīmikaḷ koluviruntu pārāṭṭukkaḷ peṟum yukattil nallaṉa nikaḻtal arumi.”

804. “maruntākap payaṉpaṭum kōpam, tītaṉṟu. varavēṟkattakkatu.”

805. “ovvoruvarum tam āṟṟal muḻuvatiyum uḻippāka māṟṟiṉāl vaḷam perukum."

806. “kūṭṭuṟavu, poruḷātāra vaḷarcci iyakkam maṭṭum alla. aḵtu or āṉmīka vaḷarcci iyakkamum ākum.

807. “eṅku, poṟuppukaḷ pakirntaḷikkap peṟavilliyō, aṅku uḻippu muḻumiyākātu, kālakkēṭum uḻippuk kēṭum irukkum.”

808. “uḻippu, uḻippukkākavē-vēli, vēlikkākavē eṉṟu ceytalē oḻukkam.”

809. “oḻukkattiṉ tāy nalla uḻippu.”

810. “aḻukkāṟu, āṇavap pōṭṭikaḷ ākkattik keṭukkum.”

811. “nilam uḻippukkēṟṟa payaṉi oḷikkāmal vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.”

812. “payaṉpaṭuttāta nili pāḻ. payaṉpaṭāta maṉitaṉum pāḻ.”

813. “arppaṇippu niliyil vāḻum tāvara iṉaṅkaḷ pōṟṟutalukkuriyaṉa.”

814. “vēḷāṇmiyil namatu muṉṉōr piṉ paṟṟiya muṟikaḷ tīmi payavātu, nalam ceytaṉa: atāvatu iyaṟki uraṅkaḷ etir viḷivukaḷi uṇṭākkātu.”

815. “paṇam-ūkkattilum paṇpāṭṭukkuriya aṟa ūkkam uyarntatu.”

816. “uṟpatti nukarvukkē ilāpattiṟku alla.”

817. “nōyiṉṟi vāḻa, kāykaṉikaḷē ciṟanta uṇavu.”

818. “maṇṇil kuṟi illi! enta maṇṇiyum maṉitaṉ payaṉpāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu varalām.”

819. “maṇṇil viḷiyum poruḷkaḷil maṉitaṉukkup payaṉpaṭāta kaḻivukaḷiyē maṇ virumpukiṟatu. kaḻivup poruḷkaḷiyum ākkamāka māṟṟum āṟṟal maṇṇukku irukkiṟatu.”

820. “maṉitaril kaṭiyāya maṉitari mēmpāṭuṟac ceytalilēyē ñāṉiyiṉ arumi veḷippaṭum.”

821. “nilam camūkattiliruntu kuṟivākap peṟṟuk koḷkiṟatu. niṟiya tiruppik koṭukkiṟatu. ituvē niyati. maṉitaṉ camūkattiliruntu niṟiya eṭuttuk koḷḷavē virumpukiṟāṉ.”

822. “pēṇappaṭupavaḷ peṇ; maṟṟavarkaḷip pēṇip pātukāppavaḷ peṇ.”

823. maṉitaṉ eḷitil maṟakka maṟukkiṟāṉ, āṉāl pala naṉmikaḷi maṟantu viṭukiṟāṉ. naṉṟallātati maṟakka maṟukkiṟāṉ.”

824. “peṇkaḷukkē, peṇṇip perumip paṭuttat teriyavilli.”

825. “peṇkaḷ, tam iṉappaki uṭiyavarkaḷ.”

826. “māppiḷḷikku (āṇukku) vāykkum māmiyār nallavaḷ! āṉāl peṇṇukku appaṭip paṭṭa māmiyār vāyppatu aritu.”

827. “maṉitaṉ eḷitil uṇarccivacappaṭak kūṭātu. kāraṇa, kāriyaṅkaḷi ārāyntu pārkka vēṇṭum.”

828. “maṉitaṉ taṉṉuṭiya periya tavaṟukaḷik kūṭa niyāyappaṭuttukiṟāṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷuṭiya ciṟiya piḻiyikkūṭa peritupaṭuttukiṟāṉ.”

829. “jaṉanāyakam veṟṟi peṟa araciyal vātikkuk koḷki, kōṭpāṭu uṟuti ākiyaṉa tēvi.”

830. “peṇ perumikkuriya piṟappu.”

831. “etirpārttuc ceytal eṉpatē ciṟanta ceyaṟpāṭu.”

832. “vitīkap pūcaṉiyi viṭa, āyiram maṭaṅku payaṉuṭiyatu aṉpuvaḻipāṭu.”

833. “ceytit tāḷkaḷ tarum ceytikḷik koṇṭu naṉmi, tīmikaḷi muṭivu ceyya iyalātu; kūṭātu.”

834. “kuṭiyāṭciyiṉ ciṟappu, kuṭimakkaḷukkum aracāṅkattiṟkum uḷḷa nerukkamāṉa uṟavukaḷip poṟuttatēyām.”

835. “aracāṅkattiṉ nitiyip pōṟṟat teriyaviṭṭāl valimiyāṉa aracāṅkam amiyātu.”

836. “naṉmikaḷē kūṭa piṟitoru kālattil tīmiyāka māṟi viṭuvatuṇṭu. viḻippu tēvi.”

837. “kūṭṭuṟavu iyakkaṅkaḷ, pañcāyattukkaḷ nāṭṭi uyirppu niliyil vittuk koḷḷa utavi ceyvaṉa.”

838. “vivacāyikaḷukkuk kaṭaṉ vajā! maṟupuṟam kaḷḷukkaṭi vacūl!”

851. “eḷiyar, uyarvu pārāṭṭutalum, mika uyarvuṭiyōr tammi eḷiyarākkik koḷḷutalum ulakattil kāṇappeṟum kāṭci.”

852. “uṟavi vaḷarttup pēṇutal peruvāḻ viṉit tarum.”

853. “pukaḻil mayaṅkāta māṉiṭar illi. pukaḻvatil tārāḷamāka naṭantu koḷvatu uyarvataṟku vaḻi”

854. “uṭalōṭu muraṇpaṭṭu viḷiyāṭiṉāl nōyiṉāl taṇṭittu viṭukiṟatu.”

855. “iḻivāka eṇṇi alaṭciyam ceytāl, etirviḷiyāka aṟivit tarukiṟatu, uṭal.”

856. “ovvoru ceyalukkum ataṟkuriya payaṉik kaṇakkiṭal uḻippiṉi ūkkuvikkum”

857. “intiya vāḻkkiyamippil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa cātip piriviṉikaḷ eḷitil nīṅkātu. piriviṉi vaḻippaṭṭa pakiyit taṇittālē pōtum.”

858. “iṉṟu paraspara nampikkiyaṟṟavarkaḷē kūṭṭāḷikaḷ.”

859. “avaravar viṉivaḻi avaravar” eṉṟa viti immiyum piṟaḻāmal nikaḻvati es.ṭi.es-em.ji.ār. mōtalil uyttuṇara muṭikiṟatu.

860. “takuti uṭiyavarkaḷiviṭat takutiyillātavarkaḷē uyara virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

861. “ovvoru paḻakkamāka naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyil uṟutippaṭṭālkūṭa eḷitil veṟṟi peṟalām.”

862. “potu makkaḷ ēmāḷikaḷāka irukkum vari āṭciyāḷarkaḷ yōkkiyarkaḷāka māṭṭārkaḷ.”

863. “paṇattiṉ matippu, allatu vili orē mātiriyāka irukkum eṉṟu karutit tiṭṭamiṭutal kūṭātu. paṇa vīkkam kavaṉattil irukkavēṇṭum.

864. “uṟpattiyāḷaṉāka māṟiṉāl oḻiya vāḻkkit taram nilittu niṟkātu.”

865. “oruvari maṟittu oruvar collum coṟkaḷik kēḷātoḻika!

866. “taṟcalukiyaṟṟa cuyamatippīṭṭilum ciṟanta āciriyaṉ illi.”

867. “uṇarvu pūrvamāṉa ceyalmuṟippaṭṭa camūka vāḻvu tōṉṟiṉāl camutāyam naṉṟāka amiyum.”

868. “āravāra araciyalil uḷḷa kavarcci ākkap paṇikaḷukku iruppatilli.”

869. “vināṭikaḷik kaṇakkiṭṭuk kāriyaṅkaḷ ceytalē vāḻum muṟi.”

870. “aṉṟāṭa vāḻkkiyil tirukkōyiliyum camūkattiyum iṇittu vāḻtalē ciṟanta vāḻvu.”

871. “uyirppum uṇarvum ceyalil kalakkumāṉāl veṟṟi uṟuti.”

872. “tiṭṭamiṭum poḻutē etirkāla varalāṟṟup pōkki uyttuṇarntu tiṭṭamiṭal nallatu.”

873. “taṉ kāriya nōkkuṭiya cuyanalamikaḷ, aṭuttavar vīṭṭi iṭittut taṉ vīṭṭik kaṭṭuvar.”

874. “naṭimuṟic cillaṟic celavukaḷil kavaṉam mūlataṉattik kāppāṟṟa utavum.”

875. “kaṇṇukkum karuttukkum varātu maṟintu koṇṭirukkum paṇikaḷit tēṭic ceyvatēpaṇi.”

876. “uṉṉiṭamuḷḷa paṇikaḷil nī ceyya vēṇṭiya niliyāṉatit tērnteṭuttu uṭaṉ cey.”

877. “piṟar colvatik kēṭṭuc cintikkātavarkaḷ mūrkkarkaḷākavē iruppar.”

878. “namakku vāyppuk koṭukkāmalē nammītu paḻi cumattupavarkaḷ uṇṭu. viḻippāka iru.”

879. “namatu ceyaṟpāṭṭil - maṟṟavarkaḷ uṇarvu pūrvamāka īṭupāṭu koḷḷac ceyvatilēyē veṟṟi irukkiṟatu.”

880. “nallavarkaḷ vallavarkaḷāka iruntāl tāṉ, kāriyam nikaḻum.”

881. “cātāraṇamāka makkaḷ eḷitil uṇmi nilikku varuvatilli.”

882. “cuvi, uṇṇum kiḷarcciyit tarukiṟatu. āṉāl, naṉmi payappatilli.”

883. “pulāl uṇavit tavirkkak kūṭiya vaḻipirāṉṉikaḷi viruppuṭaṉ vaḷarttalēyām.”

884. “ūkkattuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟāta vēli, ari vēli.”

ta-7

885. “ēḻikaḷ, eḷitil etiyum viṟpar.”

886. “iṉṟiya āciriyarkaḷukkup poṟuppu uṇarvu vantuviṭṭāl nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam uṟuti.”

887. “jaṉanāyaka amippu eṉpatu kalantu pēcuvatu eṉpatu. āṉāl, anta amippiṟkkuḷḷēyē oruvari oruvar purintu koḷḷāmalēyē palar uḷḷaṉar.”

888. “iṉiyaṉa pēcutal ellāvaṟṟiyum kūṭṭuvikkum.”

889. “kālatti oḻuṅkuppaṭutti - kālattiṟkuriya paṇikaḷiyum oḻuṅkuppaṭuttiṉāl niṟiyap paṇikaḷic ceyyalām.”

890. “paṭippāṟṟal uṭiya kalviyē kalvi.”

891. “kaṟṟuk koṇṭē iruppatu aṟi āṟṟiṉit tuārkkāmal tantu koṇṭirukkum.”

892. “toḻiṟ puraṭci ēṟpaṭum nāṭē vaḷarum - vāḻum.”

893. “pukaḻntē kāriyam cātittuk koḷḷum makkaḷ putticālikaḷ.”

894. “taṟkālikat tēvikaḷi ottip pōṭṭāl nīṇṭa kālat tēvikaḷi aṭiyalām.”

895. “celvatti mutalīṭākkum muyaṟciyē muyaṟci.”

896. “camūkattil tāykku irukkum poṟuppu uṇarvu, tantikku varuvatilli."

897. “acaṭṭut tuṇiccal - avalattiyē tarum. aṟivārāynta tuṇivu veṟṟikaḷik kuvikkum.”

898. “piṟarukkuk koṭukkātē eṉakkuk koṭu eṉpavaṉ, tēvikkuk kēṭkavilli, aṭuttavaṉukkuk kiṭikkak kūṭātu eṉpataṟkākavē kēṭkiṟāṉ.”

899. “viruppattuṭaṉ uḻittalē uyar vāḻvukku aṭittaḷam.”

900. “camutāya māṟṟaṅkaḷ nikaḻttum āṟṟalē payaṉuṭiya āṟṟal.”





901. “iṉṟiya tērtal muṟiyil-puttarāl kūṭa lañcam vāṅkāmal irukkamuṭiyātu.”

902. “enta oru tīmiyum - marunteṉat toṭaṅkip paḻakkamākiviṭṭaṉavē yām.”

903. “veḷippaṭiyāka namatu uṟavic collik koḷḷa veṭkappaṭupavarkaḷ cuya lāpattukkāka maṭṭumē uṟavu kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

904. “āciriyar, māṇākkari aṟivāl vaḷarkkum kaṭami uṭiyavar.”

905. “cūḻnilikaḷ mītu paḻi pōṭukiṟavarkaḷ ceyalaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

906. “cūḻnilikaḷi etirttup pōrāṭuvatē vāḻkkiyiṉ vaḷarccikku utti.”

907. “palaṉ etirpārkkāmal ceyyum paṇiyē paṇiyākum.”

908. “mūṉṟukāl oṭṭam eṉṟu oṉṟu uṇṭu. uṭaṉpāṭillātavari aṉittuk koṇṭu vāḻkkiyi naṭattuvatu pōṉṟatu.”

909. “vāḻkkiyil taṭikaḷ ēṟpaṭuvatu vaḷarkkum aruḷippāṭēyām.”

910. “paṭittavarkaḷē camūkanikaḻviṟkup poṟup pēṟkiṟārkaḷ. iṉṟiya camūka nikaḻvu naṉṟāka illi. ātalāl, āciriyarkaḷ tam paṇiyic ciṟappākac ceyyavilli.”

911. “āṅkilam ulakap potumoḻi eṉpatu uṇmiyalla.”

912. “kaṭavuḷ nampikki, vāḻkkikkut tuṇi ceyyāta poḻutu nāstikam tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

913. “nāstikam kaṭavuḷ illi eṉpatilli. āriya mata nūlkaḷi ārāyntavarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta paṭṭam.”

914. “māṇavarkaḷiṭattil tāyiṉum iṉiya pācam illātavarkaḷ āciriyap paṇiyil veṟṟi peṟa iyalātu.”

915. “nalla nūlkaḷi kaṟpatu avaciyam.”

916. “kīti, varṇa vēṟṟumikaḷik kāppāṟṟum nūl.”

917. “pāratam ātipattiya caṇṭiyi vivarikkum nūl.”

918. “eriyāta vēḷvit tīyi muyaṉṟu eriya vittu maṉaniṟivu koḷvatu pōla, camutāya cīrtiruttattilum muyaṟci tēvi.”

919. “caṭaṅkukaḷāl eḷitil makkaḷ kūṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

920. “kālan tāḻtti varuvatu keḷaravam eṉṟāṉa piṟaku nāṭu uruppaṭātu.”

921. “iṟiyaruḷāl kūṭṭuvikkum maṉam. ātalāl tirumaṇam.”

922. “māyā vāta, peḷatta, camaṇak kalappiṟkup piṟakē tamiḻar vāḻkkiyil vāḻkkiyip paṟṟiya iḻivu uṇarvu tōṉṟiyatu.”

923. “tanti-tāyār koṇṭāṭuvatu piṟanta nāḷ. maṉivi koṇṭāṭuvatu tirumaṇa nāḷ. camutāyam koṇṭāṭuvatu payaṉ kaṇṭa nāḷ allatu potuvāka iṟanta nāḷ.”

924. “araciyalvātikaḷ cuṟṟulā varāta kirāmaṅkaḷil vēli ceyvatu eḷitu.”

925. “aṇṇā kiyil putiya a.ti.mu.ka. koṭi. ettaṉi koṭikaḷittāṉ avar piṭippār!”

926. “payiṟciyil vaḷarvatu paṇpāṭu.”

927. “peṇkaḷāl orumippāṭṭi vaḷarkka muṭiyum.”

928. “āṇukku aṉpuṭiya māmaṉum māmiyum vāyttu viṭukiṟārkaḷ! peṇṇukku appaṭi vāyppatilli.”

929. “kātal malarvataṟkuriya vāyilkaḷ iṉṟu illi.”

930. “oru nilippaṭṭu oruvarukkut tuṇiyāka arppaṇippu uṇarvuṭaṉ vāḻvatu kaṟpunili.

931. “ceṉṉi nakarattil maṭṭum 119000 ēḻikaḷ vāḻkiṟārkaḷ”.

932. “aḻivu - kaḻivu ulakattiyaṟkiyaṉṟu. etiyum paṭippāṟṟal uṭiyatākkalām.”

933. “nammic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa uṟpatti cātaṉaṅkaḷi muḻumiyākap payaṉpaṭuttum pāṅku vantu viṭṭālē vaḷam koḻikkum.”

934. “akattiṉ vaṟumiyē puṟattu vaṟumiyip paṭikkiṉṟatu.”

935. “tērtalil veṟṟi peṟuvatiyē nōkkamāka uṭiya araciyalvātikaḷ nāṭṭi uyartta māṭṭārkaḷ,”

936. “calukikaḷ camutāyatti vaḷarkkum caktiyākā.”

937. “tamiḻiṉam, eṉṟum orumippaṭṭalli.”

938. “uṭaṉpiṟappu, irattattiṉ irattam ākiya coṟkaḷi nikaḻvukaḷ poruḷaṟṟatākki viṭṭaṉa.”

939. “oḻuṅkukaḷ nalam payappaṉavēyaṉṟit tuṉpam taruvaṉa alla.”

940. “viti vilakkukaḷ viṉi viḷikkalaṉkaḷēyām.”

941. “piṟar varunta vāḻtal aṟamākātu.”

942. “kīḻāṉa maṉitarkaḷikkūṭa aṅkīkāram aḷippataṉ mūlam tiruttalām.”

943. “paṭittavarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭṭāl pār muḻuvatum tiruntiviṭum.”

944. “maṉitaṉukku iyaṟki vaḻaṅkiya mutal mūlapporuḷ kālamēyām. mutal uṟpattik karuvi uṭalēyām.”

945. “iṉṟu tīya paḻakkaṅkaḷ mūccuk kāṟṟāki viṭṭaṉa.”

946. “ceyalkaḷ payaṉtarā. ceyalkaḷukkuriya nōkkaṅkaḷē payaṉtarum.”

947. “uṇarccikaḷ nīriṟkumiḻi; uṇarvukaḷ alikaḷ; ārvam ūṟṟu.”

948. “maṉitaṉiṉ mutal naṇpaṉ cuṟucuṟuppē.”

949. “oṉṟumē ceyyātavaṉi viṭa, payaṉaṟṟa vikaḷic ceypavaṉ paravāyilli.”

950. “camūkattiṭam pōkātē; pōṉapiṟaku tayaṅkātē”

951. “uṉṉiṭam maṟṟavarkaḷip paṟṟi kuṟi kūṟukiṟavarkaḷiṭam viḻippāyiru. iṭam koṭukkātē.”

952. “celvattic celavaḻitta vakiyum, aḷavum, uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ pārppatu vīṇ celavit tavirkkavum, celvattic cērkkavum, tuṇi ceyyum.”

953. “poruḷuṟpattik kuṇam vaḷarntālē viyakam vaḷarum; vāḻum.”

954. “uṭal tēviyi īṭu ceyyum vāyum vayiṟum ulakameṅkum orē niliyiṉavāka amintiruppatē cōcalica camutāya amippiṉ niyāyattiṉi valiyuṟuttukiṟatu.”

955. “uḻippil pātiyē nukartaṟku uriyatu eṉpatāl iraṇṭu ki; oru vayiṟu.”

956. “poṟuppi uṇarttukiṟavarkaḷ potiyic cumappārkaḷ.”

957. “taṉimaṉitarkaḷukkiṭiyil uruvākum pakimiyil kūṭa oruvar oruvari aḻikka vāyppillāmal taṭuppatē aracu.”

958. “irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭil nerukkaṭikaḷi araciṉ tuṇi koṇṭu māyāvātam tamiḻ neṟiyi muṟiyaṭikkiṟatu.”

959. “avaravar nalaṉil uḷḷa akkaṟi aḷavukku, avaravar vaḷarccikku aṭippaṭiyāka amiyum potu nalaṉil akkari ēṟpaṭṭāl ulaku ceḻikkum.”

960. “marattiliruntu koytu eṭukkakkūṭātu, kaṉi. koyyakkaṉi'yāyiṟṟu.”

961. “ovvoruvarum taṉittaṉiyē avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum aṉpi aṟiyavēṇṭum eṉṟu ārvam kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. kālam, ottuḻippatilli.”

962. “etirppu nallatu; etiri nallataṉṟu.”

963. “tīmi ceyya valimi avaciyamilli. ciṉṉac ciṉṉap pūccikaḷ kūṭa nōyi perukku kiṉṟaṉa.”

964. “uṭal varunta uḻittālē, nōy, aṇukātu.”

965. “āṅkila vittiya muṟiyil pattiyam. illi. pattiyamillā vittiyam nōyit taṇikkalām. nōyiṉ kāraṇaṅkaḷ nīṅkā.”

966. “ēḻikaḷukkut tuṉpam paḻakip pōṉatāl muṉṉēṟṟattip paṟṟik kavalippaṭa maṟukkiṟārkaḷ.”

967. “paṇam campātippavarkaḷ tam kāriyattilēyē kuṟiyāka iruppārkaḷ; nām ēmāṟak kūṭātu.”

968. “celvattiṉ arumi teriyātavarkaḷ celaviṉaṅkaḷip paṟṟi kavalippaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

969. “coṉṉatic ceyyavē naparkaḷik kāṉōm. cuya cintaṉiyuṭiyavarkaḷ eṅṅaṉam kiṭippārkaḷ?”

970. “kaṭaṉik kaḻippatu eṉṟa uṇarvil ceyyap peṟum vēli payaṉ tarātu.”

971. “maruntumali, maruttuvariṭam ceṉṟatu pōl civāccāriyārkaḷ mantira māyaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.”

972. “kavali’kku iṭam taruvatu aḻivukku vaḻi.”

973. “karu uṟṟirukkum tāy, piracava vētaṉikku āḷāṉālē kuḻanti piṟakkum; atupōla muṉṉēṟa virumpukiṟavarkaḷ pōrāṭṭa uṇarvuṭaṉ kaṭumiyāka uḻikkavēṇṭum.”

974. “vēli vāṅkuvativiṭa vēli ceyvatu kaṭiṉam.”

975. “peṇ valiyavē aṭimiyākiṟāḷ.”

976. “vayiṟu illātu pōṉāl palar uḻikkavē māṭṭārkaḷ.”

977. “maṟṟavar tolli aṟiyātavarkaḷ mūṭarkaḷ.”

978. “kālatti aḷantu celavaḻippōrē paṇatti eṇṇi vāṅkum urimiyuṭiyavar āvar.”

979. “kālaṅkaṭanta kaṭamikaḷ cumiyāki viṭum.”

980. “iṉṟu paṭṭaṅkaḷukkum paṇpāṭṭukkum uṟavilli.”

981. “tiṭṭamiṭṭap paṇikaḷukkuc celavaḻittalē muṟiyākum.”

982. “muntirik koṭṭi pōl turuttikkoṇṭu muṉvarupavarkaḷ, kaṇkāṇikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ.”

983. “kāriya cātaṉiyē cātaṉi.”

984. “taṉṉuṭalukku, tāṉē maruttuvar.”

985. “ākātati aṟavē maṟantiṭutal naṉṟu.”

986. “cātāraṇa maṉitaṉkūṭa uṭaluḻippukku kūccappaṭukiṟāṉ.”

987. “paḷḷik kūṭam, tirukkōyilip pōl pēṇap peṟutal vēṇṭum.”

988. “āciriyar-māṇākkarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭu muyaṟciyilēyē kalviyiṉ tiṟaṉ amiyum.”

989. “vāḻkki muḻutum nūl paṭikkum paḻakkam toṭariṉ, iṉpam payakkum.”

990. “maṉappāṭam maṭṭumē kalvi eṉṟa nili amiyiṉ, cintaṉi ūṟṟu, tūrntu pōkum.”

991. “nēriṭiyākak kalvi pōtippativiṭa, ceymuṟikaḷ mūlam paṭippittal nallatu.”

992. “eḻucci niṟinta paṭippāṟṟal mikkatē kalvi.”

993. “āciriyarkaḷiṉ poṟuppiṉmiyiṉālēyē, camūka varalāṟṟil taram kuṟintu varukiṟatu.”

994. “maṉitaṉiṉ mutalum, muṭivumāṉa tēvi aṟivē.”

995. "paṭippaṟivi viṭa, paṭṭaṟivu atikap payaṉ tarum.”

996. “puttakaṅkaḷilum pōtaṉāk karuvikaḷ vili matippuḷḷavi.”

997. “vakuppaṟikkuḷ pōtippativiṭa vakuppaṟikku veḷiyē niṟiya kaṟpikkalām.”

998. “uṇmiyik kaṇṭupiṭittal eḷitaṉṟu.”

999. “cūḻnilikku iriyātal vilaṅku iyal. cūḻniliyit taṉakku ēṟṟavāṟu amittuk koḷḷap pōrāṭutal maṉita iyal.”

1000. “uṭampiṟku uḻippiṉmi, nōy. uṟavukku, aḻukkāṟu nōy.”





1001. “oṟṟumi nallatutāṉ! āṉāl, etaṟkāka oṟṟumi? aṭutta camūkattiṉ mītu tākkutal ceyya oṟṟumi uruvāṉāl naṉṟaṉṟu. nāṭu, ūr aḷavil oṟṟumi uruvātalil oraḷavu naṉmi uṇṭu.”

1002. “tuṉpaṅkaḷit tavirttuk koḷḷātavaṉ aṟivillātavaṉ.”

1003. “pāratam” toṭarkiṟatu ottivippatil.”

1004. “kiyūṭṭukaḷ koṭuttu kāriyam cātikkum vacatiyuḷḷavarkaḷ uḷḷavariyil, pūṭṭiṉi oḻikka muṭiyātu.”

1005. “aracu aluvalakaṅkaḷ cuṟucuṟuppāka iyaṅkiṉāl kiyūṭṭit tavirkkalām.”

1006. “tērtalil paṇam viḷiyāṭuvatit tavirttālē, nāṭu vāḻum.”

1007. “araciyalvātikaḷ vāṅkum ilañcamē, nāṭṭil ilañcatti vaḷarkkiṟatu.”

1008. “vilivāci ēṟṟam araciṉ ceyaliṉmiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1009. “aṉṟu irāvaṇaṉ cītiyit tūkkiyatu pōl, caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ tūkkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.” illi, irāvaṇaṉ tūkkiya cīti pativirati, “caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷ appaṭiyallavē!”

1010. “makkaḷāṭci naṭipeṟum nāṭṭil aracu iyantiratti palavīṉappaṭuttak kūṭātu; aracu aluvalarkaḷ matikkappaṭutal vēṇṭum.”

1011. “iṉṟu intiyāvil 90% pērukku vāḻtal kuṟikkōḷ alla. piḻippu naṭattavē virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

1012. “taṭikaḷik kaṭantum toḻil ceyyum uṇarvu niṟinta uḻippukkup peyar muyaṟci.”

1013. “iṉṟiya nāṭṭil aracukku untucaktiyāka viḷaṅkupavarkaḷ viyāpārikaḷē! ataṉāl vili ēṟukiṟatu.”

1014. “vāḻnāḷ muḻutum paṭittal vēṇṭum, iṭiyīṭiṉṟip paṭittal vēṇṭum.”

1015. “poṟuppi ēṟkat tayaṅkukiṟavarkaḷ yātoru paṇikkum payaṉpaṭamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1016. “oḻuṅkamivu peṟṟa vāḻkki veṟṟi.”

1017. “camūka nalaṉukkākat taṉṉi aḻittuk koḷkiṟavarkaḷ pākkiyavāṉkaḷ.”

1018. “kacappuṇarcciyum kāḻppuṇarcciyum illāta camutāyam kāṇum muyaṟci tēvi.

1019. “vili pōkiṟavarkaḷ uḷḷa vari turōkam oru toṭarkati.”

1020. “keṭṭikkārat taṉattil nūṟu kāriyam ceyvativiṭa oru nalla kāriyam iyalpākac ceyvatu perumikkuriyatu.”

1021. “tamiḻiṉa varalāṟṟil iṉa orumip pāṭṭukkāka vāḻntavar pēraṟiñar aṇṇā oruvar tāṉ.”

1022. maṟṟavarkaḷip pukaḻumpoḻutu taṉṉiyum iṇittuk koḷpavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷ pukaḻa vāḻa iyalātu.

1023. “oru talivaṉukkuc ciṟanta niṉivu avartam koḷkikaḷi naṭimuṟippaṭuttuvatutāṉ.”

1024. “nōy iyaṟkiyaṉṟu; ceyaṟkiyē.”

1025. “tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷ kālappōkkil uṭaliṉ ceṅkurutiyiyē naccut taṉmi aṭiyac ceytuviṭukiṟatu.”

1026. “aṉpiṉāl niṟinta itayam uḷḷavarkaḷ, cirittu makiḻntu vāḻvārkaḷ."

1027. “kaṭavuḷi nampāmiyē oru pāvamaṉṟu. maṟṟavarkaḷip paṟṟi kavalippaṭāta ācikaḷē pāvattiṉ pāṟpaṭṭaviyākum.”

1028. “malarukkuk kēṭu ceyyāmal, tēṉi tēṉi eṭukkiṟatu. atupōlac celvatti īṭṭatal vēṇṭum.”

1029. “matam, toḻilākavum piḻippākavum māṟiyataṉ viḷivu nāstikam.”

1030. “nāḷtōṟum vāḻpavarkaḷ eḷitil mutumiyaṭivatilli. palar nēṟṟu maṭṭumē vāḻntavarkaḷ.”

1031. “nalla cuṟṟam amital, pukaḻ mikka vāḻvukku aṭippaṭi.”

1032. “orāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku kūṭat tamiḻ marapukaḷ pēṇamuṭiyāta aḷavukkup palavīṉap paṭukiṉṟōm.”

1033. “caṅkaramaṭam, camutāya aḷavil mēvi varuvatu, tamiḻ marapu vaḷarccikkut tuṇi ceyyātu.”

1034. “kītiyum tirukkuṟaḷum namatu maṟi eṉṟu kūṟuvatu pētimi.”

1035. “makkaḷ, ilakkiyam, kali, iciivaṟṟi aṉupavikkak kaṟṟuk koṇṭāl nallavarkaḷ ākiviṭuvārkaḷ.”

1036. “kuṭumpa nalat tiṭṭattiṟku celavaḻikkum tokiyi, kali vaḷarccikkuc celavaḻittāl nalla kuṭumpam iyalpākavē amiyum.

1037. “vitīka pārppaṉiya camayattiṉar tam koḷkikku māṟākiya tēvāraṅkaḷi mūṭi maṟittu viṭṭaṉar.”

1038. “irācarācaṉ tirumuṟikaḷi mīṭṭatiṉ mūlam tamiḻ nākarikattiyē mīṭṭuk kāttāṉ.”

1039. “tājmakāl, kātali niṉivil tōṉṟiyatu. tañcip periya kōvil mātēvaṉ niṉivil tōṉṟiyatu.”

1040. “ceytip parimāṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟivi vaḷarkkum, nalleṇṇattit tōṟṟuvikkum; iḻappukkaḷ varātu taṭuttuviṭum.”

1041. “tiṭṭamiṭutal eṉpatu kālam, poruḷ, āṟṟali muṟippaṭutti vīṇākāmal tavirkkavē.”

1042. “muṉṉē pukaḻavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpukiṟavarkaḷ, uṇmiyil pukaḻukkuriyavarkaḷ allar.”

1043. “celvattit toṭarcciyāka īṭṭum pāṅkil nirvakippatē nalla niti nirvākam.”

1044. “tuḻnilikūṭat taṟcārpit tōṟṟu vikkum. cūḻniliyic cārnta taṟcārpu tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1045. “maṟṟavarkaḷuṭiya palavīṉam, taṉatu palam eṉṟu niṉippatu tavaṟu.”

1046. “tamiḻaracarkaḷ āṭciyamippi valimippaṭutta, kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyi makkaḷiṭiyē parappiṉārkaḷ.”

1047. “nukarum poruḷkaḷāka, ūtiyam vaḻaṅkappeṟṟāl uṟpattip perukum; paṇavīkkamum kuṟiyum.”

1048. “celvam, celvākku iraṇṭum ērāḷamāṉa naṇparkaḷik kūṭṭit tarum. itaṉāl payaṉilli. tuṉpattil tuṇiyāy amivōrē naṇparkaḷ.”

1049. “poṟuppuṭaṉ paṇiyiṉi ēṟkātavariṭam paṇikaḷi oppaṭippatu taṟkolikkuc camam.”

1050. “piṟar muyaṉṟu peṟṟa paṭippiṉiyiṉ vaḻi, pāṭam kaṟṟuk koḷpavar eṇṇikkiyē mikuti.”

1051. “poruḷātāra ēḻmikkukkut tāy uḻippiṉmiyē!”

1052. “nalvāḻkkiyiṉ tēvikaḷikkūṭat tiṭṭamiṭṭu aṭiya muyaṟcippār illi.”

1053. “ellārukkum conta vīṭu kaṭṭik koḷḷa āciyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl poruḷ īṭṭum ārvamilli!”

1054. “kaṭanta kālam kaṭantatē. kavalippaṭṭu payaṉ illi. iṉivarum etirkālam namatu kiyil illi. atip paṟṟik kavalippaṭuvatu poruḷaṟṟatu. ātalāl namatu vacattil irukkiṟa nikaḻkālattip paṟṟik kavalippaṭuka.”

1055. “makkaḷukkup poruḷiṉmēl āciyirukkiṟatu. āṉāl, uḻippiṉ mēl āci illi.”

1056. “entac cūḻniliyilum nampikkiyi iḻantuviṭakkūṭātu.”

1057. “camūka mēmpāṭṭup paṇikaḷi mēṟkoḷpavarkaḷ tīvira kaṭci cārpukaḷi mēṟkoḷḷutal kūṭātu.”

1058. “talivar kāmarācar avarkaḷ tōṟṟa 1967kkup piṟakē nām araciyal kaṭcip paṇikaḷil īṭupaṭavilli. pārāṭṭutalukkuriya ṭākṭar kaliñar nammi mēlcapi uṟuppiṉarāka niyamaṉam ceyvatil ārvam kāṭṭi utaviyapōtu kūṭa, araciyal paṇikku aḻittārē tavira, kaṭci araciyalukku īṭupaṭutta virumpavilli.”

1059. “kuṟikkōḷ vaḻippaṭṭa ceyaṟpāṭukaḷ āḻappaṭumpoḻutu akalam kuṟikiṟatu. itu viruppu, veṟuppiṉ pāṟpaṭṭatalla. ituvē iṉṟiya namatu nili.”

1060. “putiyaṉa kāṇum ārvamē vaḷarccikku vittu.”

1061. “taṉṉic cērntārit tirutta iyalāmal maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu ciṉamuṟutal tavaṟu. ceyalāṇmiyaṟṟa nili.”

1062. “makkaḷi vaḷarccikku āyattappaṭuttutal mutaṟpaṇi.”

1063. “makkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmiyi etir nōkkil tiruttutal iyalātu. avarkaḷ makiḻum uṇavu, uṭi, mutaliyaṉa vaḻaṅkiṭum uṭaṉpāṭṭup paṇikaḷ mūlamtāṉ iyalum.”

1064. “taṉṉi oḻuṅkuppaṭuttik koḷvatil veṟṟiyiṉ irakaciyam potintuḷḷatu.”

ta-8

1065. “āravāramāka pēcuvatu” - cuṟucuṟuppāṉa vāḻkki eṉṟu karutuvatu tavaṟu. ceyaṟpāṭṭil tāṉ, cōmpaliṉmiyik kāṇa iyalum.”

1066. “oṉṟi, koḷkiyeṉa vivātattiṟku vippatē tavaṟu eṉṟāl, karuttu vaḷarcci ēṟpaṭātu.”

1067. “kuṟukiya vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ vāḻpavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷātal aritu.”

1068. “tāṉ koṇṭa muṭivukaḷiyē vaṟpuṟutta niṉippavarkaḷiṉ cevip pulaṉ vēli ceyyātu.”

1069. “kūṭṭu vāḻkki ciṟappuṟa, maṟṟavar colluvatit taṭiyiṉṟik kēṭka vēṇṭum eṉṟa nōkkilēyē taṭiyilāc cevikaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa.”

1070. “cila kūṟutalum pala kēṭṭalum vāḻkkikku nallatu.

1071. “potuvāka māṉuṭac cātiyiṉ aṟivārnta poṟikaḷ pulaṉkaḷ aṟivārnta niliyil iyaṅkāmal nukarvu niliyilēyē iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.”

1072. “cintittal, āyvu ceytal, kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa virintu nikaḻavēṇṭum eṉpataṉālēyē uṭalamippil aṟivup poṟikaḷ. nīṇṭa iṭiveḷi ivikaḷukkiṭiyil illi.”

1073. “vayiṟum pāluṟuppukkaḷum iyaṅkum aḷavukku mūḷi iyakkam peṟātatu maṉita kulattiṉ kuṟiyē."

1074. “taṭpa veṭpa nilikaḷ uṭal nalamuṭi yōrukkut tīṅku ceyvatilli.”

1075. “piriviṉikaḷum vēṟṟumikaḷum uḷḷa variyil, maṉitakulam amitiyāka vāḻiyalātu.”

1076. “kuṟukiya pātikaḷ payaṇattiṟku ēṟpuṭiyaṉa ākā! atupōlak kuṟukiya nōkkaṅkaḷ vāḻkkip payaṇattiṟkup payaṉtarā.”

1077. “urimiyiyal caṭṭam aṉivarukkum oṉṟātal nallatu.”

1078. “intu camutāyattil nilavum piriviṉikaḷ vaḻi, cilar ākkam aṭikiṉṟaṉar. ataṉāl, oṉṟu paṭuttum paṇi eḷitaṉṟu.”

1079. “namatu camutāyak kaṭṭamippil astivāram cantēkam.”

1080. “makaḷir, taṅkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkiyil peṟaṟkariyatu urimi eṉṟu karutiviṭṭāl viṭutali kiṭittuviṭum.”

1081. “kalvit tiṟaṉum poṟuppum uṭiya māṇavarkaḷ paḷḷikku varāmalē kaṟṟuk koṇṭu viṭuvar.”

1082. “cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnili cariyilli eṉṟu kūṟukiṟavarkaḷ pērāṭṭa uṇarvillāmal piḻippu naṭattupavarkaḷ.”

1083. “etaṟkum camātāṉam collamuṭiyum. āṉāl, ēṟṟukkoḷḷa iyalātu.”

1084. “iṉṟiya āciriyarkaḷippōla mūḷic cōmpēṟikaḷ eṉṟum iruntatilli,”

1085. “oru ceyalik kaṭami uṇarvuṭaṉ ceytāl atu aḻakākavum payaṉtarat takkatākavum amiyum.”

1086. “eḷitil celvam kiṭittāl uyir vaḷarātu; takuti: peṟātu.”

1087. “tuṉpam niṟinta ceyaṟpāṭukaḷē payaṉum paṇpāṭum niṟinta vāḻkkikku aṭippaṭi.”

1088. “muḻuk kavaṉattuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟāta paṇikaḷ uriya makiḻvit taruvatilli.”

1089. “ovvoruvarum avaravarkaḷukkuk kiṭitta vāyppukaḷi naḻuvaviṭāmal payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭāl muṉṉēṟalām.”

1090. “tēṭum paḻakkam aṟivi vaḷarkkum. āḷviṉi āṟṟalit tarum.”

1091. “celvattic celavukkāka eṉṟu tēṭutal kūṭātu. iruppukku eṉṟu tēṭutal vēṇṭum.”

1092. “keṭṭa maṉitarkaḷ erimali pōla uḷ pukintu koṇṭē iruppar.”

1093. “uṭal, uyir, aṟivu mūṉṟum camaniliyil vaḷarntālē maṉitakulam mēmpāṭu aṭiya muṭiyum.”

1094. “ulakil vaṟumikku kāraṇam uḻippiṉmiyum uḻippukku uriya pāṅkiṉmiyumē yākum.”

1095. “uttaravātamuḷḷa vāḻkkiyilēyē kātal ciṟakkum.”

1996. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam poṟuppuk koṭuppatu eḷitu. āṉāl poṟuppu ēṟpatē muṟi.”

1097. “cāmpirāṇip pukiyi nukarntāl kēṭu: pukiyiṉ maṉatti maṭṭum nukarntāl nalam.”

1098. “pakuttaṟivu, pēccup poruḷ alla; atu oru vāḻkki muṟi.”

1099. “eppōtum uḻippukkuriya āyatta niliyil iruppatē-viḻittiruttal.”

1100. “pala nāḷ uḻāta nilam pāṟiyākiṟatu. palanāḷ aṟivāl uḻappaṭāta maṉitar pulaṉkaḷ kallākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.”





1101. “caṭṭatti eḷitil vimarcaṉam ceyyalām; āṉāl ēṟṟu oḻukutal kaṭiṉam.”

1102. “caṭṭaṅkaṉ naṉmiyi vaḷarkkum taṉmi uṭiyaṉa alla; tīmiyi taṭukkum nōkka muṭiyaṉavēyām.”

1103. “pūṭṭukkaḷi nampukiṟavarkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ yōkkiyat taṉmiyi nampuvatāka naṭippatu ēṉ?”

1104. “aṉpum, aṟaṉum kalavāta vāḻkkiyil piṟakkum kuḻantikaḷi vaḷarkka, ciṟanta paḷḷikaḷum-ūrum tēvi.”?

1105. “ācippaṭukiṉṟaṉar! āṉāl, āḷumikku añcukiṉṟaṉar.”

1106. “uḻittu vāḻāta vāḻkki, vipacāram.”

1107. “uṭal uṟuppukkaḷil oṉṟu kuṟintālum caṅkaṭappaṭupavarkaḷ, tirunta vēli ceykiṟārkaḷ illiyē!”

1108. “poruḷātāra ātipattiyap pōṭṭikaḷ ulakan taḻuviyatāka vaḷarntu varukiṉṟatu.”

1109. “cārntē vāḻpavarkaḷ, naṭuniliyāḷarkaḷi vāḻa aṉumatikka vēṇṭum.”

1110. “intiya araciyal vātikaḷ ātāyam tēṭum maṉappōkku, cōram pōkum nili ākiyavaṟṟiṉ kāraṇamāka intiyāviṟku jaṉātipati āṭci muṟi tēviyākiviṭum.”

1111. “cātāraṇa payaṉpaṭāta kuṭiciyik kūṭa yārum iḻakkat tayārāka illi.”

1112. “poṟikaḷi, iṉpa nukarvil pakkuvappaṭuttuvatu kali.”

1113. “evvaḷavu tiṟamiyākak kāriyam cey tālum toṭarcci illiyāṉāl payaṉ illi.”

1114. “kālamaṟintu ceyyāta kaṭamikaḷ uriya payaṉi tārā.”

1115. “oru karuttil ūṉṟātavarkaḷ cuṟṟic cuṟṟi varuvar.”

1116. “iṟivaṉ vāḻttup poruḷ alla. vāḻvup poruḷ.”

1117. “iṉṟu vāḻkiṉṟavarkaḷ, varalāṟṟup pōkkil vantaminta vāḻkki. amippukkaḷi aṭutta talimuṟiyiṉarukku appaṭiyē viṭṭu vittal kaṭaṉākum.”

1118. “civattiṟku ciṟappu cērkka nālvar vaḻipāṭu tēvi.”

1119. “pakimi pārāṭṭāmal vāḻtal oru ciṟanta paṇpu.”

1120. “talimikku ācippaṭātu maṟṟavarkkut talimiyi viṭṭuttarum tiriyacālikaḷ āyiram āṇṭukku oru taṭavitāṉ piṟakkiṉṟaṉar.”

1121. “aṉṟāṭam kaṇakkup pārkkātavaṉ ākkam peṟa iyalātu.”

1122. “kālam eṉṟa kaḷattiṉ arumiyi iṉṉamum palar aṟiyātatu, intiya camūka vāḻkkiyiṉ tīmikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.”

1123. “aṭiyavēṇṭum eṉṟa muṉippuṭaṉ kūṭiyatē muyaṟci.”

1124. “kāriyat tēvikaḷ, maṉitaṉi nirvāṉa nilikkut taḷḷiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.”

1125. “mamatiyiṉ muṉṉāl ciṟu tavaṟukaḷum periyaṉavākat tōṉṟum.”

1126. “nām ākki vittāl pōtātu. ūṭṭi viṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum cilar etirpārkkiṟārkaḷ.”

1127. “cintiyil akanti uṭiyavarkaḷ eḷitil aṇukamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1128. “muntirip paḻattil koṭṭi veḷiyē turuttik koṇṭiruppatāl paḻattiṟkuc cuvi kuṟivu.”

1129. “mariyāti’ eṉṟa peyarāl viḷiyum maṉaccaṅkaṭaṅkaḷē mikuti.”

1130. “maṉa eḻucciyuṭaṉ pēcupavarkaḷ nilittu niṟkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1131. “coṉṉatic coṉṉapaṭi niṟivēṟṟu pavarkaḷē uḻippāḷikaḷ.”

1132. oḻuṅkumuṟi ākkattiṟku aṭippaṭi.”

1133. “piracava vētaṉi illāmal tāy illi. atupōla tuṉpappāṭu illāmal iṉpam illi.”

1134. periya vēlikaḷ naṭipeṟat taṭiyāyiruppavi, ciṉṉa ciṉṉa vēlikaḷ muṟiyākac ceyyap peṟāmiyē!”

1135. tāyiṭam aṉpu pārāṭṭuvatē oru kali tāṉ.”

1136. “koḷkip piṭivātam vāḻkkiyi vaṟaṭcit taṉmiyuṭiyatākkiviṭum.”

1137. “illāmiyāl ulaku aṭiyum tuṉpatti viṭa āciyāl paṭum tuṉpamē mikuti.”

1138. “uyiruṭaṉ koṇṭu cellum aṟivu, paṇpukaḷil uḷḷa ārvattiviṭa uṭaṟcārpāṉa ācikaḷē ulakatti āṭṭippaṭikkiṉṟaṉa.”

1139. vēliyil īṭupāṭu, kuṟikkōḷ iruntālē varum.”

1140. oru kālattil naṉmiyākak karutap peṟuvatu nilitta naṉmiyāki viṭuvatilli.”

1141. “eṉakku naṇparāka iruntāl pōtātu. eṉatu pakivarukku virōtiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu etirpārppatu virumpattakka talla.”

1142. “uṇarcci niṟinta vēkattil irukkum poḻutu aṟaneṟi upatēcam payaṉpaṭātu. anta niliyil marunteṉa uṇarccikku iri pōṭutal payaṉtarum. āṉāl āpattāṉa paricōtaṉi.”

1143. “uṭaṟpayiṟci kāraṇamākavē palar vēli ceyya niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukku uḻippu nōkkam alla.”

1144. “etiyum piṉ toṭarcciyākak kavaṉikkat tavaṟiṉāl keṭum.”

1145. “niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ, iyakkanili eytutal vēṇṭum.”

1146. “uṭal ōyātu iyaṅkukiṟatu. atupōla vāḻkki ōyātu iyaṅkutal vēṇṭum.”

1147. iyaṟki amittut tarum kaṉikaḷilum maṉitaṉ camikkum uṇavu nallaṉa alla.”

1148. “uṇmiyāka naṭattal eṉpatu akamum puṟamum ottu naṭattal eṉpatē!”

1149. “intiya camūkap piriviṉikaḷ, tīmikaḷ nīṅkiyē yākavēṇṭum.”

1150. “kuṟikkōḷi aṭiyum vāḻkkikkut tuṉpattit tuṇiyākak koḷḷum tuṇivu tēvi.”

1151. “namatu makkaḷāṭcimuṟi maṉu eḻutum paḻakkattiyē kaṟṟut tantuḷḷatu.”

1152. “uḷam niṟi īṭupāṭē uḻippukku ūṟṟu.”

1153. “kārl mārks matatti etirkkum poḻutu, anta iṭattil oru putiya matatti vāḻkki muṟiyi aṟimukappaṭuttiṉār. ataṉāl oraḷavu veṟṟi peṟṟār. āṉāl, periyār etirppi maṭṭumē ceytār. ataṉāl nilittu niṟkavilli. avar pācaṟiyil vaḷarntavarkaḷēkūṭa kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭukku vantuviṭṭaṉar. atu maṭṭumalla. matatti etirttup pēcutal aṟiyāmiyiṉ ceyal eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.

1154. “camayam, camūkam, iraṇṭum iṇinta nōkkaṅkaḷē! inta iṇippu parāmarikkap peṟṟālē camutāyam vaḷarum”

1155. “intiya araciyal paṭumōcamāṉa nilikkuppōyk koṇṭirukkiṟatu. veṟi nāy nilikku vantuviṭṭatu.

1156. “enta oṉṟilum iṟukap paṟṟiya cārpu vantuviṭṭāl nīti pulappaṭātu.”

1157. “ulaka māṉiṭa cāti ‘cutantira’ varalāṟṟi uṇarntālē cutantaratti iḻakkat tuṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1158. “ceyalaṟṟavarkaḷ, ceyaluḷḷavarkaḷ mītu aḻukkāṟu koḷḷutal tavirkka iyalātatu, kavalaṟka.”

1159. “āḷumi eṉpatu oru kali. ati nāḷtōṟum payilutal vēṇṭum.”

1160. “ceyaṟpāṭṭukkuriya āyatta niliyil irukkap paḻakiyavarkaḷ ciṟanta āḷumiyip peṟuvārkaḷ.”

1161. “aṟiviyal, poruḷiyal, āṭciyiyal, aruḷiyal aṉittum vīṭṭu vācaṟpaṭikku vantālum ēṟka muṉvarā cila apūrvap piṟavikaḷ uṇṭu.”

1162. “kāmam-aṟivārnta iṉpam varavēṟkattakkatu. kāmam kaḷiyāṭṭamāyiṉ taram kuṟiyum.”

1163. “oru kuḻantikkum peṟṟōrātal peṟaṟ kariya pēṟu.”

1164. “maṉaveḻucci aṭaṅkiya niliyē pūcakar ātalukkut takuti.”

1165. “tuḻnilikaḷi etirttup pōrāṭuvatē uytikku vaḻi.”

1166. “ulakantaḻiiya oṭpam peṟutalē iṉṟu mutaṟkaṭami.(oṭpam-mēṉmi)

1167. “naṉmikaḷukkellām naṉmi aḻukkā ṟiṉmiyē.”

1168. “kāmam’-uyirkaḷukku viruppam niṟinta vāḻkkiyit tūṇṭuvatu.”

1169. “tuyttal pāpam illi. tuykkum pāṅkiyal tāṉ puṇṇiya, pāpam karuk koḷkiṟatu.”

1170. “ceyyum ceyalkaḷ payaṉ taruvatilli. nōkkaṅkaḷē payaṉit taravallaṉa.”

1171. “vaṉtaṉmi’ - koḷḷutal vāḻkki veṟṟikaḷik keṭukkum.”

1172. “tiruttamuṟac ceypavarkaḷ kiṭikkātu pōṉāl nāmē ceytuviṭuvatu nallatu.”

1173. “yār iṟantāl eṉṉa? caccaravu niṟkumā?”

1174. “kuṭumpa uṟavukaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarvatē nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭi,”

1175. uyirkaḷ maṉa vikāramiṉṟic ceḻittu vaḷartalē nākarikattiṉ aṭippaṭi.”

1176. “cuḻalum irāṭṭiyākiviṭṭatu iṉṟiya māṉuṭa vāḻkki.”

1177. “kaṭci jaṉanāyakam illiyāṉāl āṭciyil amarpavarkaḷ keṭṭup pōvārkaḷ.”

1178. “uṇarvu pūrvamāka oṉṟic ceyya muṉvarātavarkaḷiṭam paṇiyi oppaṭippatu tavaṟu.”

1179. “iṉṟiya iḷiñarkaḷ ācippaṭukiṉṟaṉar. āṉāl aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉiyil viruppamilli.”

1180. “uḻippāl āḷa ulakattil uḻippiṉi viliyākat tarāmal vāḻa niṉippatu kuṟṟam.”

1181. “kuṟivākac ceytālum muṟiyum oḻuṅkum taḻuviya ceyalmuṟi tēvi.”

1182. “paṇam tēviyirukkiṟatu. paṉam varum vāyilum irukkiṟatu. āṉāl, eṭuppārittāṉ kāṇōm.”

1183. “payiṟci peṟāta uḻippāḷikaḷ ceyvārkaḷ. āṉāl pōtiya payaṉ irukkātu.”

1184. “kūṭṭattik kuṟi! uḻippiṉ taratti uyarttuka.”

1185. “vitimuṟikaḷ, ceṉṟa kālap paṭippiṉikaḷ īṉṟa kuḻantikaḷēyām.”

1186. “taṟkāppu uṇarvu” muṉippāka iruppavarkaḷ kalantu pēca maṟuppārkaḷ.”

1187. “enta oṉṟiyum tīra āyvu ceyyāmal-ceyvatu iḻappi tarum.”

1188. “nirvākap pōkkil āyvu ceyvatu-vaḷarccikkut tuṇi ceyya inta āyvukku ‘nampikki’ eṉṟa tattuvam taṭiyāka amital kūṭātu.”

1189. “oru paṇiyil oruvari toṭarntu īṭupaṭuttutal-kēṭṭukku vaḻi vakukkum.”

1190. “taṭi illāta vaṇṭiyum āyvu illāta nirvākamum vipattukkaḷit tōṟṟu vikkum.”

1191. “āci-āttiram takutikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟuvatilli.”

1192. “iṉam - moḻiyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟi nilavum vaḻakku-itu ulaka vaḻakku cāti mata aṭippaṭiyil, iṉam iruttal iyalātu, kūṭātu”

1193. “cāti’ piṟappiṉpāṟpaṭṭatu. itu ayal vaḻakku: tamiḻ vaḻakkaṉṟu. nītikkum vaḷarccikkum puṟampāṉatu.”

1194. “kulam’ vaḻi vaḻi varum marapu. itu ēṟpuṭiyatu. marapu vaḻiyum cūḻniliyiṉ tākkamum tavirkka iyalātaṉa.”

1195. matam-maṭam ākiya amippukkaḷ appaṭṭamāṉa cātiyamippukkaḷē! iṅkellām putumiyi etirpārttal iyalātu.

I196. “mēyppār illāta māṭupōla kaṇkāṇippu illāta nirvākak keṭum.”

1197. “oruvariṭamē pala kālattukku orē paṇi iruppatu naṟpaḻakkattit tūṇṭuvatilli.”

1198. “teḷivākak karuttup parimāṟṟam illiyāṉāl kāriyak kēṭuvarum.”

1199. “paṭittavarkaḷiṭam uḷḷa tīyap paṇpukaḷ akaṉṟālē-camutāyam uruppaṭṭuviṭum.”

1200. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ pakti nammuṭiya peṭṭiyit tīṇṭa aṉumatikkakkūṭātu.”





1201. “maṟṟavar cevvi pārttu oḻukutal uyariyap paṇpu.”

1202. “tērtaliyē nōkkamākak koṇṭa araciyal, makkaḷip piccik kārarākkiviṭum.”

1203. “aracu tarmaccāliyāka māṟiviṭak kūṭātu.”

1204. “ellām iruntum kāriyam naṭakka villi. ēṉ? itutāṉ maṉitariṉ viparītamāṉa pōkku.”

1205. “palanāḷ paḻakiyavarkaḷ paṇpil talip piriyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1206. “iṉṟiya āḷuṅkaṭciyiṉariṉ aṭittaḷattiṉar nāṭē taṅkaḷuṭiyatu eṉṟa muṭivukku vantu āṭṭam pōṭukiṉṟaṉar. intap pōkku etir viḷivukaḷi uruvākkuvatu iyaṟkiyiṉ niyati! tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1207. “tāḻttappaṭṭōr, piṟpaṭṭōr, muṟpaṭṭōr paṭṭiyal oru nāṭṭil niliyāka iruppiṉ atu nītikkum vaḷarcci eṉṟa niyatikkum māṟupaṭṭa camutāya amippākum.”

1208. “matippu, matiyāti eṉpatu vili kūṭutalukkuriyatu eṉṟa nili poymmiyi vaḷarkkum”

1209. “kālam, uḻippu, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum poruḷkaḷ ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭu matippukkuriyaṉa. tirumpak kiṭikkāta etuvum iḻappiyē tarum.”

1210. “vāḻkkiyum oruvaki viyāpāramē. vāḻtalukkuriya celavi viṭa aṭital kūṭutalāka vēṇṭum. ituvē ūtiyam niṟinta vāḻkki.”

1211. “paṇpāṭillātavar atikāram peṟutal, kuraṅku kiyil koḷḷi peṟṟatu pōlākum.”

1212. “nirvākam eṉpatu kālam, iṭam, poruḷ, uḻippu ivaṟṟi muṟippaṭuttip payaṉ paṭuttip payaṉ koḷḷutalākum.”

1213. “kōppukaḷ kuṟikkōḷuṭiyaṉavāka iyakkap peṟātu pōṉāl iḻappēyām.”

1214. “kaṇṇāl kāṇpatu, kātāl kēṭpatu ākiyaṉa mūlam toṭarcciyāṉa ceyalūkkattip peṟātavarkaḷ iruntālum iṟantavarkaḷēyāvar.”

1215. “cariyāṉa nērattil cariyāṉa takavalit tarutal nirvākattiṟku valimiyic cērkkum. ākkattiṉit tarum; iḻappukkaḷit tavirkkum.”

1216. “mukkāli mūṉṟukāluṭiyatu. itil orukāl uṭintālum payaṉ illi. atupōlak kūṭṭamippil taṉi oruvariṉ palavīṉam kūṭa pātippukaḷit tarum.”

1217. “aṟiviyal, uḻippi eḷimip paṭuttik koṇṭu varukiṟatu. āṉāl vāḻkkiyi arumippaṭuttik koṇṭu vrukiṟatu.”

1218. “mākkam, viyappu ākiyavaṟṟiṟkuriyaṉa vāḻkkiyi pūraṇattuvam ceyyā.”

1219. “urimi uṇarvē niliyāṉa uṟavukku aṭippaṭi.”

1220. “oruvar veṟukkappaṭutalukkuk kāraṇam avartam iyalpukaḷ maṭṭumalla. maṟṟavarkaḷ eṭukkum muṭivukaḷum kāraṇam ākum.”

1221. “ovvoruvarum avaravar vāḻkkiyi muṟippaṭuttik koṇṭāl yārukkum iṭarppāṭu illi.”

1222. “oru camūka māṟṟam eḷitil nikaḻātu. toṭarcciyāṉa pōrāṭṭam tēvi.”

1223. “enta oru tīmiyum toṭakkattil tayakkattuṭaṉ talikāṭṭukiṟatu. piṉ paḻakkattiṉ kāraṇamākat tīmiyē iyalpākiviṭukiṟatu.”

1224. “cāmārttiyamum tiṟamiyum oṉṟalla.”

1225. “cāmārttiyam eppaṭiyum kāriyatti cātippatu. tiṟami, muṟippaṭi cātippatu,”

1226. “appāvikaḷ nallavarkaḷallar. tīmi ceyya caktiyaṟṟavarkaḷ.”

1227. “nallavar'-itu muḻu niṟic col. āṟṟaluṭiyavarkaḷē nallavarkaḷāka vāḻtal iyalum.”

1228. “māṉam illātavarkaḷ enta tavaṟukaḷukkum varuntamāṭṭārkaḷ. tiruntavum māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1229. “aṭikkaṭi ēṟpaṭum celavukaḷil viḻippāka iruppatu niti nirvākattiṟkut tēvi."

1230. “eṇṇey tēyttuk koṇṭu piṉ cīyakkāy koṇṭu kaḻuvi viṭuvatu pōl, tuyppit tuṟavāl kaḻuvavēṇṭum.”

1231. “cittāṭukiṟavarkaḷ nām virumpuvatik koṭuttāl nampalām.”

1232. “ūkkam eṉpatu ceyalūkkaṅkaḷēyām.”

1233. “oṉṟiyoṉṟu piriyāta niliyil piṇikkappeṟṟa piṇippē vāḻvaḷikkiṉṟaṉa. atupōla camūkam piṇikkap peṟṟāl tāṉ vāḻvu ciṟakkum.”

1234. “tavaṟukaḷ ceyvatum viḷiyāṭṭāki viṭṭa viparītam vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.”

1235. “poṟuppu uṇarvu kaṭiccarakkaṉṟu. vāḻviṉ paṭippukaḷāl peṟuvatu.”

1236. “uyarvaṟa uyarum niliyē nili. ituvē varavēṟkattakkatu.”

1237. “paṭippaṟivilum paṭṭaṟivu ciṟantatu.”

1238. “kāytal-kuḷirtal eṉṟa niyatikaḷi poruḷkaḷi māṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, kāytalum kuḷirtalum vāḻkkiyi vaḷarppaṉa.”

1239. “oḻuṅkāka amikkap peṟṟa paṟkaḷē uṇavi aṟikka utavukiṉṟaṉa. atupōla oḻuṅkamivupaṭṭa uḻippē uṇarvukaḷi vaḷarkkum; ākkattiṉi nalkum.

1240. “uṭalamivil mutaṉmi, iraṇṭām nili eṉṟa nili enta uṟuppukkum illi.

ta-9 etuvum cariyāka iyaṅkātupōṉāl taliyum keṭum. ātalāl, ovvoṉṟum muṟiyāka iyaṅkutalē vēṇṭum.”

1241. “uyirppuḷḷa viḻippu niliyil camutāyam iyaṅkiṉāl vāḻnili ciṟakkum.”

1242. “peṇ, oru pōkap poruḷ eṉṟa karuttu uḷḷavariyil peṇṇaṭimittaṉam nīṅkātu.”

1243. “cuṟṟuppuṟac cūḻnili kāraṇamāka nallavarkaḷāka iruppatum iyaṟki.”

1244. “tamiḻarkaḷ uyar cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ; āṉāl ceyalāḷarkaḷ allar.”

1245. “niṟiya īṭṭal; kuṟivākac celavaḻittal-ituvē nalvāḻkkiyiṉ niyati.”

1246. “celvam kiṭikkum poḻutu nukarvik kūṭṭik koṇṭāl, varuvāy kuṟiyumpōtu iṭarppāṭu tōṉṟum.”

1247. “kaṭamikaḷ, urimikaḷ, poṟuppuṇarvukaḷ ākiyaṉa icinta oru paṇpē, kūṭṭuṟavu eṉpatu.”

1248. “ciṟanta kūṭṭuṟavāḷaṉ colvataṟku muṉpu kēṭpāṉ; vāṅkuvataṟku muṉpu koṭuppāṉ; uṇpataṟku muṉpu uḻippāṉ.”

1249. “ākkam tēṭi, muṭiyum eṉṟa nampikkiyil “peṟamuṭiyātavarkaḷ iḻappil peritum kavalippaṭuvar.”

1250. “taṉṉip pōla maṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṟu niṉikkātavarkaḷ camūkattil vāḻvatu virumpat takkatalla.”

1251. “tēviyillāmal pēcuvatu payaṉil col kūṟutalē.”

1252. “kaṟiyāṉ tāṉāka virumpi muyaṉṟu uḻittu vīṭu kaṭṭuvatilli. āṉāl kaṭṭiya vīṭṭil eḷitil kuṭi pukukiṟatu.”

1253. “kaṭṭiya vīṭṭiṉ arumi teriyāta kaṟiyāṉ ati aḻikkiṟatu. atupōla naṉmiyaṟi yātavarkaḷ naṉmiyi aḻippārkaḷ”.

1254. “payattiṉiṉum kēṭu piṟitoṉṟu illi.”

1255. “vāyiṉāl muṭikkakkūṭiya kāriyatti eḻuttiṉāl ceyyakkūṭātu.”

1256. “toḻilil iḻappu eṉṟu kūṟi, toḻilāḷarkaḷukku nalla ūtiyam koṭukka maṟuppatu nalla nirvākam alla.”

1257. “nirvākam eṉṟa amippē, cīruṟa naṭattavē!”

1258. “etiyum poṟuppuṭaṉ ceyya muṉ varātavarkaḷ, maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu paḻi pōṭuvar.”

1259. “palarōṭu paḻakuvatāl utavikaḷ ceyvatilum koṭiyaḷippatilumkūṭa cikkal tōṉṟum.”

1260. “ovvoru uṟavum poṟuppukkaḷiyum kaṭamikaḷiyum koṇṭatēyām.”

1261. “uṟavukaḷ aṉittum etirpārppukaḷi uṭiyaṉavē. etirpāāppukaḷic ceyya iyalāta pōtu maṉattāṅkal ēṟpaṭum.”

1262. “ceyyum maṉamiruntāl vāyppukaḷ tāmē vanteytum.”

1263. “pukaḻ-mēlāṇmi-ceyaltiṟaṉ ākiya naṉmitaru tuṟikaḷilkūṭa aḻukkāṟu tōṉṟāmal taṭukka, taṉṉaṭakkam tēvi.”

1264. “kuṟinta viliyuṭiya uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷē nalla uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉa.”

1265. iyaṟkiyi oṭṭic ceṉṟāl etuvum ceyyalām. iyaṟkiyiliruntu māṟupaṭum vāḻ nili kūṭātu.”

1266. “ceyaṟki, iyaṟkiyōṭu poruntātu.”

1267. “nalla camiyaṟkārar kiṭittuviṭṭālē āyiram kāriyam ceyyalām.”

1268. “namatu uṭalik kavaṉikkavē uṟavukaḷ: āṉāl naṭappatutāṉ illi.”

1269. “taṉṉi eṉṟum iḷamiyuṭiyōṉākat karutik koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkāka-poymmiyāka uṭaliṉ niliyiṉi maṟittu vāḻtal kūṭātu.”

1270. “uṭaṉukkuṭaṉ nōykkuriya kāraṇatti māṟṟātu vāḻvatu, nōyi atikappaṭuttum.”

1271. “nōyiṉ kāraṇatti māṟṟāmal nōykku maruntuṇpavarkaḷ, nirantara nōyāḷikaḷ ākiviṭuvar.” 1272. “periya maṉitarkaḷukku nōy varuvatē oru cittiravati! taṇṭaṉi!”



1273. “pala nūṟu tuḷikaḷ cērntatutāṉ āḻkaṭal; kācukaḷākac cērkkap peṟṟāl celvamum kōṭiyāki viṭum.”



1274. “cillaṟi uṟavukaḷ celvattiṟkuk kēṭu.”



1275. “ēḻikaḷiṭattil uḷḷa aiyappāṭē muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku mutal taṭi.”



1276. “vēṇṭuvār vēṇṭuvatellām iṟivaṉ vaḻaṅki viṭṭāl, ulakam vicittira vilaṅku kūṭāramākiviṭum. ataṉāltāṉ iṟivaṉ vaḻaṅkat takkatiyē vaḻaṅkukiṟāṉ!”



1277. “kallil muḷi aṭippatum, kallātavarukku upatēcippatum vīṇ.”



1278. “ācippaṭupavarkaḷum, nirvāṇa cātukkaḷum veṭkatti viṭṭavarkaḷ.”



1279. “pataviyil cukam irukkiṟa vari pōṭṭikaḷit tavirkka iyalātu.”



1280. “niṟuvaṉattiṟkāka taṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyi arppaṇikkāta ūḻiyarkaḷik koṇṭu niṟuvaṉattik kāppāṟṟa iyalātu.”



1281. "arppaṇippu uṇarvillāta amippil, mēlāṇmi kalvi ēṟpuṭiya takkavarāka amittal vēṇṭum."



1282. “taṉakku ivar utaviyē tīrvār eṉṟa nampikkiyum kūṭa, vāḻkkiyil alaṭciyattit tōṟṟuvikkiṟatu”.





1283. “ciṟuciṟu paṇikaḷik kūṭa muṟiyākac ceyyātavarkaḷ ki niṟiya ūtiyam etirpārkkiṉṟaṉar.”

1284. “oru iṉattiṉ oṟṟumi viruppamē iṉṟu aiyappāṭṭiṟku uriyatākiviṭṭatu.”

1285. “meypporuḷ uṇartal eṉpatu vēṟu. kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu eṉpatu vēṟu.”

1286. “meyññāṉamum matamum oṉṟalla.”

1287. “meyññāṉam kāṇpatuvē vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷ.”

1288. “viññāṉam curaṇṭum varkkattukkuk kūṭa, tuṇi pōkiṟatu.”

1289. “viññāṉam iyaṟkiyiṉ uḷḷīṭukaḷik kaṇṭu vāḻkkiyiṉ aṉupavattiṟkuk koṇṭu varum ariyap paṇiyic ceykiṟatu.”

1290. “viññāṉam ēvalaṉāka iruppatu naṉṟu.”

1291. “viññāṉam talimiyāka māṟak kūṭātu.”

1292. “oruvaruṭiya camayam toṭarpāṉa nampikkikaḷ - avarkaḷuṭiya vāḻkkiyiyum - anta nāṭṭiṉ camūka vāḻviyum pātikkac ceyyum.”

1293. “intiya camūka vāḻkkiyiṉ vaṟumikku mikutiyum kāraṇam ūḻ tattuvaṅkaḷēyām.”

1294. “varicippaṭutal eṉṟa marapu nallatu.”

1295. “avaṉ - avaḷuṭaṉ ataṉip (iyaṟkiyi) payaṉpaṭutti vāḻtalē- vāḻkki."

1296. “vallāṅku vāḻtal eḷitu. vāḻvāṅku vāḻtal aritu.”

1297. “vāṉavar ulakam vaḷam niṟintatu: āṉāl, paṇpāṭaṟṟatu.”

1298. “maṉita vāḻkkikku mikavum iṉṟiyamiyātatu paṇpāṭēyām.”

1299. “vāḻkkikku mikavuyarnta kuṟikkōḷ amiyāviṭil vāḻkki ciṟattal iyalātu.”

1300. “maṟṟavar varampu ukanta cutantiratti eṭuttuk koḷvatu vāḻkkiyik keṭukkum.”





1301. “tayavilum tavaṟukaḷ nikaḻvatu uṇṭu.”

1302. “aṟivoṭum - naṭimuṟiyoṭum poruntāt tayavu naṉṟaṉṟu.”

1303. “ovvoru uyirum kaṭicit taruvāyilum kūṭa cukittiṭavē virumpum.”

1304. “malikkup pakkattil niṟpatellām maliyākiviṭātu. atupōla periya maṉitaric cērntavarkaḷ ellām periya maṉitarkaḷ illi.”

1305. “oṉṟi ūkkattuṭaṉ ceytāl eḷitil kāriyam muṭiyum.”

1306. “ceymuṟikaḷuṭaṉ pāṭam pōtikkum marapu vaḻit tiruvuruvamē ālamar kaṭavuḷ.”

1307. “caṭṭaṅkaḷ variyaṟiyil niṟkum amippukkaḷ vaḻaṅka iyalātu.”

1308. “niti mikamika uyarntatu."

1309. “ovvoru intiyaṉum matac cārpaṟṟa taṉmiyuṭiyavaṉāy iruntālē matac cārpaṟṟa nāṭu.”

1310. “poṟuppuṇarnta nili, vāḻkkiyi iyakkamākki viṭum.”

1311. “vāḻkkiyil uḷḷa cōmpalē poṟuppiṉmikkuk kāraṇam.”

1312. “iṉteṉa vēli terintiruntum atic ceyyātu cuṟṟuvatu aliyum maṉattiṉ aṭiyāḷam.”

1313. “iṟi vaḻipāṭu maṭṭumē vāḻkki eṉṟāl māṉuṭap piṟappi iṟivaṉ ceytirukka vēṇṭiyatilli.”

1314. “araciṉ tiṭṭaṅkaḷi aṟintu koḷḷutalē ākkattiṉ mutaṟpaṭi.”

1315. “celavukaḷil mutaṟ celavu cēmittal”

1316. “koṭiya tuṉpaṅkaḷiyumkūṭa makiḻvāka aṉupavikkalām-vāḻkkiyil vaḷaravēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarviruntāl.”

1317. “camutāya amippiṉ ciṟpi āciriyarē.”

1318. “ovvoruvarukkum orē oru upācaṉā mūrttitāṉ irukkavēṇṭum. entat tiruvuruvat tiṉik kaṇṭālum taṉatu upācaṉā mūrttiyiṉ vaṇṇamāka niṉippatē marapu.”

1319. “maṉacāṭci” eṉṟa col, maṉattukkuc cāṭciyāka irukkum kaṭavuḷiyē kuṟikkum.”

1320. “nām muṟṟākap poṟuppēṟka iyalāta niliyil, cattiyam ceyvatu kūṭātu.”

1321. “aṟu kayiṟu ūcaleṉṟu maṉam alital kūṭātu.”

1322. “maraṇa vāyililum vaṉkaṇmi uḷḷam māṟātavarkaḷ vaḷarcci yaṭiyātavarkaḷ.”

1323. “nām maṭṭum nallavarkaḷāka iruntāl pōtātu. nammic cuṟṟi iruppavarkaḷum nallavarkaḷāka iruntālē payaṉ uṇṭu.”

1324. “maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam mēlāṇmi koṇarvatē cariyāṉa nirvākattiṟku utaviyāka amiyum.”

1325. “aṭimip puttiyuṭiyavarkaḷ cutantaratti aṉupavikkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1326. “piḻippu naṭatta virumpukiṟavarkaḷ poṟuppuṭaṉ naṭantukoḷḷa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1327. “kālattil kaṇkāṇittāl iḻappukkaḷit tavirkkalām: īṭṭaṅkaḷik kūṭṭalām.”

1328. “cintippavaṉ, cintikkātavaṉ collik kēṭpatu pittiyakkārattaṉam.”

1329. “ciṟu ellikaḷittāṉ camāḷikka iyalum.”

1330. “kālam, poṉ-iraṇṭiyumē aḷantu payaṉpaṭutti miccam piṭikkak kaṟṟuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.”

1331. “māṉiṭa uṟavukaḷip parāmarippatu payaṉkaḷit tarum.”

1332. “pukaḻi virumpukiṟavarkaḷiṭam, kañcat taṉam illāmal pukaḻntu pēcuka.”

1333. “valimiyuṭiyār tēṭātu pōṉālum ceṉṟu uṟavu koḷvar.”

1334. “uḻikkātu uṇpavarkku iyaṟki vaḻaṅkum taṇṭaṉi tāṉ nōy.”

1335. “cila tīrmāṉaṅkaḷ eṭuttālum, ceyaṟpaṭuttappaṭuvatu nallatu.”

1336. “piḻipaṭac ceyvār varunti māṟṟik koḷḷāmikkuk kāraṇam, oṉṟu aṟiyāmiyāka irukkavēṇṭum allatu akantiyāka irukka vēṇṭum.”

1337. “ciṟiya ciṟiya tavaṟukaḷiliruntē periya tavaṟukaḷ tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa.”

1338. “paṭippikkum āciriyarkaḷ cuṟucuṟuppāka iruntāl, nāṭē cuṟucuṟuppāka iyaṅkum.”

1339. “kaṭamikaḷiviṭa kāppi, tēnīr aruntal mutaṉmip peṟṟuviṭṭāl yārtāṉ kāppāṟṟa iyalum.”

1340. “vayiṟu illātu pōṉāl palar vēli ceyyamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1341. “aṉpu illāta iṭattil makiḻcci illi!”

1342. “karuṇikku viñciya ciṟappuṭiyatu inta viyakattil illi.”

1343. “ovvoru ceṅkallāka aṭukkik kaṭṭiyatil uruvāṉatē māḷiki. atupōla ovvoru ceyalākattāṉ pukaḻpūtta vāḻvu uruppeṟukiṟatu.”

1344. “oruvar vāḻvu, piṟitoruvar vāḻviṉ nalaṉuṭaṉ piṇintuviṭiṉ vāḻvittu vāḻum paṇpu kāl koḷḷum.”

1345. “oḻuṅkukaḷ paḻakkappaṭum variyiltāṉ tolli. paḻakkattiṟku vantu viṭiṉ atu pātukāppāṉa cātaṉam.”

1346. “oruvarukku oruvar utaviyāka iruppatu eṉpatu piṟappiṉ vaḻi aminta kaṭami.”

1347. “paṭittavarkaḷ ceyta tavaṟukaḷiṉ payaṉē āṭcimuṟikaḷ.”

1348. “ciṟunīrakam cīrāka iyaṅkātu pōṉāl viḷiyum tolliyip pōṉṟē ciṟiyavarkaḷ cīrāka iyaṅkāviṭiṉum tuṉpam ēṟpaṭum.”

1349. “malaccikkalilum koṭiyatu maṉaccikkal. virivil tīrvu kāṇpatu nallatu.”

1350. “tām ceyyātu viṭum kāriyaṅkaḷāl ulakam periya tuṉpattiṟku āḷāvati aṟika.”

1351. “veṟṟu araciyal, ārppāṭṭattil veḷiccam pōṭukiṟatu.”

1352. “naṉmiyik kūṭa, naṉmikkākavē ceyyavēṇṭum. nipantaṉikaḷukku kaṭṭuppaṭṭu ceyyak kūṭātu.”

1353. “poruṭ celvam pōṟṟum paṇpaṟṟavarkaḷ poruḷuṭiyarātal aritu.”

1354. “mikavum tarattil kuṟinta maṉitarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷiyum tam aḷavukōliyē vittu aḷappar.”

1355. “camūkac cēviyil evvaḷavu makiḻvu irukkiṟatō avvaḷavu nerukkaṭiyum irukkum.”

1356. “nāḷukkoru toḻiṟkūṭam kaṇṭāl nalivu nīṅkum.”

1357. “nirvākiyiviṭa uḻippāḷarē uyarntavar.”

1358. “intiya camūka amippil nirvākikaḷi viṭa, toḻilāḷikaḷ kuṟivāṉa ūtiyamē peṟukiṟārkaḷ.”

1359. “cutantaram eṉpatu aṉupavikkat takkatu. āṉāl cutantaratti aṉupavikkum tiṟaṉ eḷitil varuvatilli.”

1360. “atikārattāl oṉṟic ceyvativiṭa, aṉpāṉa aṇukumuṟiyāl ceyvatu payaṉuṭiya paṇpāṭṭu muṟi.”

1361. “kūṭṭuṟavu eṉpatu, irukkum piriviṉikaḷik kaṭantu oṉṟupaṭuvatu.”

1362. “iṉṟu kiyūṭṭu tēciyamayamāki viṭṭatu.”

1363. “viruppam-viḻivu ivaṟṟil piṟantu vaḷarnta kiyūṭṭu iṉṟu kaṭṭāyamāki viṭṭatu.”

1364. “maṉitarkaḷ eṭutta tīrmāṉaṅkaḷ ceyaluruk koṇṭiruntāl ulakam corkkamāki irukkum.”

1365. “ceyaṟpāṭṭukku varāta, aracu āṇikaḷē mikuti.”

1366. “toṭarpuṭiyār karuttaṟiyāmal oru kōppiṉi muṭippatu tavaṟu.”

1367. “maṉitakulam oṉṟē! ituvē iyaṟti! piriviṉikaḷ iyaṟkikku māṟāṉavi.”

1368. “maṉitakulam muḻumiyaṭiya mutaṟpaki vaṟumi."

1369. “vaṟumi ēṟṟattāḻvu - ivikaḷi vāyilkaḷākak koṇṭē paki tōṉṟukiṟatu.”

1370. “varalāṟu toṭarntu iyaṅkukiṟatu. inta varalāṟṟiyakkattik kūrntaṟintu kaṟpavarkaḷ, vāḻkkiyil veṟṟipeṟuvārkaḷ.”

1371. “intiya cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ tuṟavu maṉap pāṉmiyuṭiyōrāy, aracuk kaṭṭilukku varāmiyiṉālēyē namatu nāṭṭuc cintaṉiyāḷarkaḷ tōṟṟārkaḷ.”

1372. “puttar kūṭa tuṉpa nīkkattiṟku vaḻi tuṟaveṉṟē kaṇṭār.”

1373. “oru poruḷiṉ matippik kūṭṭuvatu uḻippu.”

1374. “taṅkam vili matippuṭiyatutāṉ. āṉāl, attaṅkattiṉ matippaṟiya, cātā matippuṭiya māṟṟukkal tāṉ payaṉpaṭukiṟatu. atupōla, uyarnta cintaṉikaḷ kūṭa carācari maṉitariṭattil tōṟṟuvikkum tākkaṅkaḷip poṟuttē matippiṭap peṟum.”

1375. “vaḻivaḻi varum paḻakkaṅkaḷukku oru aracāṅkattiviṭa atika celvākku uṇṭu.”

1376. “intiya cutantarap piracavamē palavīṉamāṉa kaṣṭa nilitāṉ!”

1377. “āṅkilēyarkaḷ pōyviṭṭālum, avarkaḷ vititta iṉa, moḻi, camayap piriviṉi naccu vitikaḷ ceḻittu vaḷarkiṉṟaṉa.”

1378. “tīvira camayavātikaḷukku inta ulakatti viṭa aṭutta ulakattip paṟṟiya kavaliyē atikam.”

1379. “paḻakkam eṉpatu cēṟu. iccēṟṟil cikkiyavarkaḷ eḷitil kariyēṟa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1380. “teriyavēṇṭiyaṉa teriyavum, tuṇiya vēṇṭiyaṉa tuṇiyavum ceyya vēṇṭiyaṉa ceyyavum taṭiyāka iruppavi etuvum tīmiyēyām.”

1381. “ceyaliṉmiyāl yāṉ aḻivativiṭa tāṭcaṇyattāl aḻivatē mikuti.”

1382. “kūṭṭuc camūka vāḻkkikkut taṉṉit tayārākkik koḷḷātavarkaḷ kūṭṭattil iruntālum taṉi maṉitarē!”

1383. “ulakam muḻutum camanilic camutāyam tōṉṟiṉālē pōriṭum ulakam tiruntum.”

1384. “vāḻvōriṉum cettār uṇṭu; cettōriṉum vāḻvōr uṇṭu.”

1385. “akkaṟi eṉpatu iṭam nōkkic ceyaṟpaṭum paṇpaṉṟu; iyalpāṉatu.”

1386. “kācukaḷiṉ arumi aṟiyātār, eppōtum celvantar ākār.”

1387. “mēṭikaḷ amittalē ippōtu oru piḻippākiviṭṭatu.”

1388. “irupālum otta, oṉṟik kūṟiṉālum ēṟka muṉ varātu pōṉāl avarkaḷ niyāyatti maṟukkiṟārkaḷ.”

1389. “cilarukku oṉṟi eppaṭiyum aṭintu viṭuvatu eṉṟa maṉattiṉ piṉṉaṇiyil karavum vaṉkaṇmiyum irukkum. ivarkaḷ uṟavu ākātu.”

1390. “uḻikkāmal cāppiṭum nilappirapukkaḷ, paṭitta aṟiñarkaḷ, pēccukkup paṇam kēṭpatik kuṟi colkiṟārkaḷ.”

1391. “oṉṟit toṭarcciyākak kaṇ kāṇikkātu pōṉāl, putiya cikkalkaḷ tōṉṟum.”

1392. “uḻāta nilam keṭuvatu pōla, paḻakāta uṟavum keṭum.”

1393. “tiṟamiyaṟṟavarkaḷ, nirvākat talimikku ācippaṭuvatu payaṉaṟṟatu.”

1394. “oṉṟum ceyyātavarkaḷiviṭa, ceyvatu pōl pāvaṉi ceykiṟavarkaḷ mōcamāṉavarkaḷ.”

1395. “kuḻukkaḷi uruvākka, cērkka muyaṟcippavarkaḷ ētō oru koḷkikku āyattam ceykiṟārkaḷ eṉpatē karuttu.”

1396. “nālupēr uṭkārntu pēcum iṭattil kūṭṭuc cintaṉi nikaḻum pōtu taṉittum pirintum eṇṇutal cittāṉiṉ ceyal.”

1397. “taṟkālikamāṉa uḻippukkaḷik kāṭṭic camāḷippavarkaḷ ciṟu tūṟalkaḷip pōlat tariyi naṉikkalām. avarkaḷāl vaḷattiyum veṟṟiyiyum taraviyalātu.”

1398. “oru paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷa palar, oruvari oruvar eṇṇattālum ceyalālum uṟavālum neruṅki varātu pōṉāl antap paṇi ciṟappāka naṭavātu.”

1399. “ēkātipattiya aracukaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭu nākarikamāka illi. koli ceyyum aḷavukku vantuviṭṭaṉar. iṉi inta ulakatti, kaṭavuḷtāṉ kāppāṟṟa vēṇṭum.”

1400. “yāri nampuvata” eṉṟa viṉā, maṉita nākarikam keṭṭu viṭṭatu eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷam.”





1401. “vēliyē payiri mēykiṟatu; pātu kāvalaṉē kolkiṟāṉ! kaliyukattiṉ yatārtta nili!”

1402. “oru talimi uruvāvataṟku evvaḷavu kālam celavu ākiṟatu? oru noṭiyil koli ceytu viṭukiṟārkaḷ pāvikaḷ.”

1403. “aṉṉi intirā, intiya nāṭṭiṉ pātukāppukkum vaḷarccikkum ceyta paṇikaḷ niṉintu niṉintu pōṟṟattakkavi.”

1404. “viḷakkāta pāttiram tūymiyāy irātu. āyvu illāta paṇiyil vaḷarcci irātu.”

1405. “kirāmac cūḻalil kuḻantikaḷ naṉku vaḷarkkappaṭṭāl mētikaḷ vaḷarvārkaḷ; tōṉṟuvārkaḷ.”

1406. “aṉṉi intirāviṭam tuṇivum uṟutiyum iruntatu! atēpōḻtu nekiḻntu koṭukkum maṉappōkkum iruntatu.”

1407. “mutalāḷittuvam” eṉṟa nañcu uḷḷa variyil nallavarkaḷ vāḻamuṭiyātu.”

1408. “mutalāḷi vēṟu; celvantar vēṟu.”

1409. “celvam illāta ēḻikaḷil kūṭa mutalāḷikaḷ uṇṭu. itu oru kuṇam.”

1410. “pala, ciṟu kaṭappāṭukaḷ muṟiyāka nikaḻntālē periya kaṭappāṭukaḷ tāmē nikaḻum.”

1411. “enta oru ciṟu nikaḻviyum kūrntu kavaṉittu vāḻkkiyi iyakkuvatu aṟivuṭimi.”

1412. “cila maṉitarkaḷ tavaṟu ceyvataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ kūṟa iyalātu. atu avarkaḷuṭiya cupāvam.”

1413. “uṭuppatu uṇpatu ivaṟṟi eḷimip paṭuttuvatu aḻukkāṟṟit tavirkka utavi ceyyum.”

1414. “oruvari aṭiyoṟṟip piṉpaṟṟāmal pōṟṟuvatu oruvitamāṉa uṇarcci. ataṉāl payaṉ illi.”

1415. “intiya araciyalil jaṉanāyakap paṇpu muḻumittuvam aṭiyavilli.”

1416. “kacappuṇarcciyum kāḻppuṇarcciyum illātavarkaḷē jaṉanāyakattil kāl koṇṭu niṟka iyalum.”

1417. “intiya araciyalil naparkaḷē mukkiyam. koḷki, kōṭpāṭukaḷ alla.”

1418. “enta oṉṟum muṟiyāka naṭavātu pōṉāl iḻappukkaḷē varum.”

1419. “pātukāppu vēlikaḷ, vēliyāka maṭṭum amiyāmal uṇarvu pūrvamāṉa kaṭamiyāka amintāl nallatu.”

1420. “jaṉanāyap paṇpukku iṉa, moḻi, mata vēṟupāṭukaḷ icintatalla.”

1421. “karuttu vēṟṟumikaḷ kacappuṇarcciyāka urukkoḷḷutal jaṉanāyaka marapukku ici villātatu.”

ta-10

1422. “oru elli variyiltāṉ poṟuttāṟṟum paṇpu tīmi ellik kaṭakkum poḻutu kayamittaṉamākiviṭukiṟatu! kolvatit tavira vēṟu vaḻiyilli!”

1423. “paṇṭum iṉṟum eṉṟum paranta maṉappāṉmi ulakiyalil aṭintatu tōlviyē!”

1424. “nallavarkaḷiviṭa tīyavarkaḷ nallavarkaḷāka naṭippatu, kavarcciyāka irukkiṟatu. makkaḷ mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.!

1425. “viṭuppil irukkum viruppam, uḻippilum iruntāl ulakam uruppaṭṭuviṭum.”

1426. “upatēciyātal eḷitu. āṉāl ceyvatu kaṭiṉam.”

1427. “maṉam oru vintiyāṉa poruḷ. muṟiyākap paḻakkiṉāl nāy; illiyāṉāl puli:”

1428. “nōy varātu vāḻtalē oru kali. ācikaḷ illāmaṟpōṉāl nōy illi.”

1429. “nūlkaḷip paṭippatu camitta cōṟṟic cāppiṭuvatip pōṉṟatu.”

1430. “iṉṟu āci, araciyal ellām veṟum uḷḷīṭillāta uṇarccikaḷēyām.”

1431. “talivariṉ koḷkikku moṭṭi pōṭukiṟavarkaḷ, talivar iṟantālum tali moṭṭi pōṭukiṟārkaḷ.”

1432. “palar ēṟṟuk koḷḷum oru ceyal makkaḷāṭci muṟi taḻuviyatē.”

1433. “vāyppukkaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vaḷarpavarkaḷ cilarē.”

1434. “kacappuṇarcciyi vaḷarttal eḷitu; akaṟṟutal aritu.”

1435. “intiya camūka vāḻkkiyil vērūṉṟiya piriviṉi eṇṇaṅkaḷi eḷitil akaṟṟa muṭiya villiyē!”

1436. “niyāyamāṉa oṉṟi aṭiya eḷiya muyaṟcikaḷē pōtum!”

1437. “piṟar paṟṟi uṉṉiṭam kuṟi colpavarkaḷ niccayam uṉṉip paṟṟiyum kūṟuvārkaḷ. viḻippāka iru.”

1438. “naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷ mūlam tāṉ tīyapaḻakkaṅkaḷi akaṟṟa muṭiyum.”

1439. “vāḻkki, kaḷippukkāka ēṟpaṭṭataṉṟu kaḷippu, vāḻkkiyil oru pakuti.”

1440. “maṟṟavar kavaliyi aṟintu māṟṟa muyalupavarkaḷiṉ kavali tāṉē maṟiyum.”

1441. “cama vāyppukaḷ iyaṟkiyāka amiyattakka vakiyil camutāya naṭimuṟi amintālē nāṭu vaḷarum.”

1442. “pāvikaḷiyumkūṭa nēcittu kaṭṭup pāṭṭiṟkuḷ vittuk koḷḷal puṇṇiyam ceytaṟku utaviyāka irukkum.”

1443. “ovvoruvrum avarkaḷāl muṭinta elli variyil taṉi naṭippōkku. iyalāta elliyil camutāyatti nāṭuvar.”

1444. “aṟpa maṉitarkaḷtāṉ” periya maṉitarkaḷākik koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.”

1445. “ciṟupāṉmiyiṉartāṉ, perumpāṉmiyiṉari aṭakkiyāḷkiṉṟaṉar. inta muraṇ pāṭṭukkuk kāraṇam aṟivu peṟutalukku aṉivarukkum cama vāyppu amiyāmiyē yām.”

1446. “eppaṭiyāvatu kāriyattic ceytu muṭittal eṉṟa vāḻkki muṟi, naṉṟaṉṟu. ippaṭittāṉ eṉṟu tiṭṭamiṭṭu neṟimuṟippaṭi amital vēṇṭum.”

1447. “tīmi ceyyātavarkaḷ aṉivarum nallavarkaḷ allar; naṉmi ceypavarkaḷē nallavarkaḷ.”

1448. “uyirukku iṉṉal viḷivikkum paṇiyi maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam ceyyak koṭuppatē maṉita iyaṟki.”

1449. “vēli vāṅkum urimiyip pōlavē vāḻvaḷikkum kaṭamiyi mēṟkoḷḷavēṇṭum.”

1450. “kālam, āṟṟal, paṇi mutaliyavaṟṟi muṟippaṭuttic ceytāl, paṇikaḷiyum niṟiya ceyyalām; mikutiyum payaṉuṭiyatākavum ceyyalām; tappāmalum ceyyalām.”

1451. “ippaṭittāṉ vāḻvēṉ eṉṟu uṇarvu pūrvamāka muṭiveṭuttuviṭṭāl namatu mūḷiyum uṭalum iyalpākavē ottuḻikkum.”

1452. “palavīṉamāṉavarkaḷāl camutāyam cīr kēṭaṭivativiṭa, palamāṉavarkaḷuṭiya palam, palavīṉatti īṭuceyyak kūṭiyatāka camutāya uṇarvuṭaṉ vaḷaravilli. paḻutāṉa iyantiratti, paḻutākāta iyantiram iḻuttup pōkavilliyē!

1453. “cilar nallavarkaḷē! tiṟami iṉmiyāl keṭṭavarkaḷ pōlat terivar.”

1454. “virivu uṇarvu vēṟu; avacarappaṭutal vēṟu. virivu uṇarvu kāriya cātaṉiyit tarum. avacarappaṭutal kāriyak kēṭukaḷiyē tarum.”

1455. “cuṟucuṟuppu vēṟu, paraparappu vēṟu. cuṟu cuṟuppu nallatu. paraparappu nallatalla.”

1456. “iṉṟiya kuṭumpaṅkaḷil pala, uṭal vayappaṭṭavi; uṇarvuvayappaṭṭavi alla.”

1457. “camamaṟṟa niliyil aṉpu tōḻami, caṉa nāyakam ciṟappatilli.”

1458. “otta nōkkillātār naṭpiṉarāy iruttal aritu.”

1459. “kaḻuvāy muṟiyāka amintāl nōy illi; paki illi!”

1460. “vallāṅku vāḻpavar toṭarntu maṟṟavar vāḻkkikkut tīṅku ceyvatit taṭukka iyalāta aracu, aracalla.”

1461. “anītiyi etirkkāmal otuṅki vāḻpavarkaḷum aniyāyakkārarkaḷē!”

1462. “aracāṅkap paṇimaṉikaḷilum eḷiya uḻippāḷikaḷukku uttaravātam illi.”

1463. “cōviyattil lañcam vāṅkiṉāṉ! itu eppoḻutō nikaḻvatu. āṉāl nam nāṭṭilō lañcam tēciyamayappaṭuttiyāyiṟṟu.”

1464. “palari moṭṭiyaṭittu vāḻpavrkūṭa tiruppatikku moṭṭi pōṭukiṉṟaṉar! pāpak kaḻuvāy pōlum.”

1465. “iṉṟiya araciyalil - vāḻat teriyātavarkaḷē peritum īṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉar.”

1466. “tēvikku vāṅkiyavarkaḷ, tiruppit tarum poḻutu caṅkaṭappaṭuvatu aṟamaṉṟu.”

1467. “nāḷi eṇṇi ūtiyam koṭukkum muṟi uḻippik keṭukkum.”

1468. “uḻippu, poruḷīṭṭal ākiya iraṇṭilum maṉa niṟivu koḷḷutal kūṭātu koṇṭāl tēkkanili varum.”

1469. “āṟṟal iyaṟki; paṭippup poruḷaṉṟu. āṟṟalip payaṉpaṭuttukiṟa muṟiyilēyē maṉitaṉ veṟṟi peṟukiṟāṉ.”

1470. “uḷḷavar peṟuvār eṉpatu āṉṟōr niyati. iṉṟu atiyum aḻukkāṟu keṭukkiṟatu.”

1471. “taṉṉiviṭa maṟṟavar kuṟivākavē peṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam iṉṟu palarip piṭittāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1472. “iṉṟu eṅkum campantā campantamillāmal pēcip peruntuṉpatti viḷivikkiṉṟaṉar.”

1473. “iṉṟiya intiyāvi, vēntalikkum kolkuṟumpu varuttukiṟatu.”

1474. “aṟivu, āḷviṉiyil viruppamillātār viḻumiya ciṟappukkaḷit tēṭi alikiṉṟaṉar.”

1475. “cāvu, - iyaṟki: āṉāl vāḻāmal vāḻvativiṭac nāmāka cāvatu mēl.”

1476. “inta ulakam vāḻkkiyiṉ mēl uḷḷa āciyāl entat tuṉpattiyum eḷitil maṟantuviṭum.”

1477. “vilaṅkukaḷ, taṉakku uṇavuk kiṭittu viṭṭāl amiti koṇṭuviṭum; maṟṟa vilaṅkukaḷip paṟṟik kavalippaṭātu inta nilikku iṉṟu maṉitarkaḷum vantuviṭṭārkaḷ.”

1478. “camanili illāta camutāyattil caṇ nāyaka tattuvam veṟṟi peṟātu.”

1479. “ceypaṇikaḷil pōrāṭṭa uṇarvu illiyeṉil vāḻkki eḷitil veṟṟiyit taḻuvātu.”

1480. “intiya camūka vāḻkkiyil mutumi, pōṟṟappaṭṭatuṇṭu. iṉṟu illi.”

1481. “paṇṭita javahālāl nēru avarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci aṟiviyal cārpuṭiyatu; cutantarat taṉmiyuṭiyatu.”

1482. “iṉṟu kālistāṉ cikkalukkuk kāraṇam pañcāpi, pañcāp, hāriyāṉā eṉṟu pirittatēyām.”

1483. “taṟkālika veṟṟikaḷukkāka tīya caktikaḷuṭaṉ oppantam ceytu koḷḷutal tavaṟu. piṉ viḷivukaḷ tīmiyiyē tarum.”

1484. “kālil muḷ tittuviṭṭatu eṉṟu kēṭkum nilikku iṉṟiya camūkam vantu viṭṭatu.”

1485. “kātal vāḻkkiyil peṇṇukkup pēruḻippu: peruñcumi! uḻippu vāḻkkiyilum appaṭiyē! itu niyāyamaṟṟa taṉmi!”

1486. “kaṭivītiyil nalla tūymiyāṉa” cattuḷḷa uṇavu niyāya vilikkuk kiṭittāl namatu peṇkaḷ vāḻkkiyi māṟṟalām; vaḷarkkalām.”

1487. “payam-kōḻittaṉattukkut tāy! narakattiṉ vāyil!”

1488. “tavaṟukaḷukku añcuvatu vēṟu. taṇṭaṉikaḷukku añcuvatu vēṟu. iṉṟu taṇṭaṉikaḷukkē añcukiṉṟaṉar. tavaṟukaḷukku yārum añcuvatilli.”

1489. “oruvartam uṭaṟkaruvikaḷi pūraṇamākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉāl iṉpamāka vāḻalām. itu cattiyam!”

1490. “vāṅkupavar vili pēcātupōṉāl, viṟpavar viruppamē viliyākiviṭum.”

1491. “oru nōkkuṭiya aracāṅkattil paṇi ceyvōriṭiyil oruṅkiṇippiṉmiyē. nōkkamillāta vāḻkkiyik kāṭṭukiṟatu.”

1492. “tiyākam, nākarikam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟik kavalippaṭātu-ātāyam paṟṟiyē kavalippaṭutal vipaccārattukku nērāṉatu.”

1493. “camamaṟṟa nilikaḷ uḷḷavariyil naṟpaṇpukaḷ vaḷartal aritu.”

1494. “vāyppukkaḷ tāṉāka varuvaṉa alla; uruvākkik koḷḷappaṭupavi.”

1495. “taṉ tavaṟṟi uṇarum tiṟaṉaṟṟavarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷ tavaṟukaḷip peritupaṭuttupavar.”

1496. “atikārattic cuvittal tīmiyil tīmi.”

1497. “perumpāṉmiyōr” muṭivellām jaṉanāyakat taṉmi uṭiyaṉavākātu!”

1498. “vivakārattil uḷappūrvamāṉa camātāṉam kaṇṭāl oḻiya-vivakārattiṟku muṟṟup puḷḷi vikka muṭiyātu.”

1499. “keṭṭikkāraṉ kiṭittatic curuṭṭukiṟāṉ; ēmāḷi vāyp paṟi aṟintu koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ.”

1500. “intiya camutāyattil putiyaṉa cintittavarkaḷ uṇṭu. āṉāl intiya camutāyam putiyaṉa vaṟṟi ēṟkavilli.”





1501. “oruvar ceyyum tavaṟi avaruṭiya camutāyattiṉ mēl ēṟṟik kūṟutal ākātu. nallatalla.”

1502. “intiyar vāḻniliyil māṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭālē-intiyā vaḷarum.”

1503. “takutiyillātaṉvaṟṟiṟku ācippaṭṭu allalpaṭutal aṟiyāmi.”

1504. “maṟṟavar tuṉpam aṟiyātār mōcamāṉa maṉitarkaḷ.”

1505. “intirā paṭukoliyil tiṭṭamiṭṭavaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟataṟkuk kāraṇam tiṭṭamiṭṭavaṉiṉ putticālittaṉamalla. maṟṟavarkaḷiṉ kaṭamiyil kāṭṭiya alaṭciyamē kāraṇamākum.”

1506. “namatu aracāṅkattiṟku cuṟucuṟuppu illāmal iruppatum orukuṟi.”

1507. “kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷi ārāyāmal pēcuvatu pittiyakkārattaṉm.”

1508. “oṉṟic ceytu muṭippatil uḷḷa tollikaḷi-maṟṟavarkaḷ uṇarvatilli.”

1509. “avacara uṇarcci aṟivik koṉṟu viṭukiṟatu.”

1510. “puki vaṇṭip peṭṭiyiṉ mītu ēṟi iruntukoṇṭa kuruvi, pukivaṇṭi pōkumpōtu tāṉ yāttiri ceytatākak kūṟa iyalātu. kuruvikku ētu aṉupavam?

1511. “uyiriṉ uṇarvukaḷi, vāḻkkiyiṉ ilakkukaḷi nōkki eṭuttuc cellum karuviyē ilakkiyam.”

1512. “uṇavukku uppup pōla ilakkiya aṉupavattiṟku racaṉi.”

1513. “nuṇmit taṉmiyuṭiya uṇarvu niliyil aminta aṉupavaṅkaḷ ippōtu illi.”

1514. “pakupporuḷiliruntu nuṇporuḷukkum ceytikaḷiliruntu cintaṉikkum cellum payiṟci ippōtu illi.”

1515. “namatu camaya niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷil paṇi ceypavarkaḷ, cēkkiḻār paṭittuk kāṭṭum kuṭumpaṅkaḷippōla vāḻntālē pōtum. mikutiyum payaṉ viḷiyum.”

1516. “iruppatil amitipeṟum-camāḷikkum iyalpu iyalāmiyiṉ viḷivēyām. camāḷittali viṭa-cātittalē ciṟantatu.”

1517. “cakipput taṉmi eṉpatu oruvakik kōḻittaṉamēyām.”

1518. “nukartal vāḻkkiyiṉ kaṭappāṭukaḷil oṉṟu; uriya vili koṭukkāmal nukartal oḻukkak kēṭu.”

1519. “maṟṟa camayaṅkaḷukku vāyttiruppatu pōla, ayal mūlataṉaṅkaḷ namakku illi. namatu mūlataṉamumkūṭa curaṇṭappaṭukiṟatē tavira-vaḷarkkap peṟavilli.”

1520. “uḻikkāmal- camūka uḻippu illāmal vāḻpavarkaḷumkūṭa oruvakiyil curaṇṭal ceypavarkaḷēyāvar.”

1521. “pūraṇattuvamāṉa uḻippu tōṉṟāta variyil viyakatti vaṟumiyiliruntu mīṭka muṭiyātu.”

1522. “kavaṉamāka - toṭarcciyākac ceyyap peṟum paṇikaḷē payaṉtarum.”

1523. “cutantaram-palar vāḻkkiyil oḷiyip parappavilli.”

1524. “maṉitakulam vāḻat terintu koṇṭāl paruva kālamum ēval ceyyum.”

1525. “kuḻantiyiṉ arumiyiyum, poṟuppiyum uṇarpavarkaḷ-kuṭumpattiṉ eṇṇikkiyi kaṭṭuppaṭuttik koḷvar.”

1526. “iṉpam eṉpatu kalaviyoṉṟiltāṉ eṉṟu vāḻum vāḻkki miruka vāḻkki.”

1527. “oru peruṅkolikkup piṟaku kūṭa oru iḷiñaṉit talivarāka orumaṉatāka tērnteṭukkiṟārkaḷ-iṅku maruttuvamaṉiyiltāṉ talivar uḷḷār-ataṟkuḷ ciṇṭupiṭiccaṇṭi!”

1528. “orumippāṭṭil vaṭapulattār vaḷarntiruppatip pōl tamiḻarkaḷ vaḷaravilli.”

1529. “intiyāvil matattiṉ peyarāl nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ-mata nampikkiyip poyttup pōkac ceykiṉṟaṉa.”

1530. “urimikaḷ peṟuvataṟku piriviṉi vēṇṭum eṉpatu poruḷaṟṟatu.”

1531. “vāḻa ācippaṭukiṉṟaṉar; āṉāl muyaṟcitāṉ illi.”

1532. “naṉṟāka vāḻtalukkut tāy aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉivē.”

1533. “oruvar mītu paṟṟu eṉpatu avarkaḷip poṟuttatu illi. avarkaḷuṭiya koḷkikaḷip poṟuttatāka amital payaṉtarum.”

1534. “iṉṟiya araciyalil kol kuṟuppuk kārarkaḷē mēvi varukiṉṟaṉar.”

1535. “ceyaliṉmi nōkki veṭkappaṭṭu varuntātār-orupoḻutum ceyal tiṟaṉukku uriyavar ākār.”

1536. “nēṟṟiya tavaṟukaḷē aḻintuviṭātu. iṉṟu antat tavaṟukaḷi niyāyappaṭuttum muyaṟciyilēyē aḻivu toṭaṅkukiṟatu.”

1537. “tiṟampaṭac ceyyātār-etaṟku? viyakattiṟkup poṟiyāka!”

1538. “parinturic ceyyum urimi-kāṟṟu pōlap payaṉpaṭuvataṟkalla; marunteṉap payaṉ paṭutalukkēyām,”

1539. “celvatti celavaḻittaliviṭa - celvākki celavaḻippatil kavaṉamum-cikkaṉamum tēvi.”

1540. “maṉampōṉa pōkkil nirvāka iyantiratti iyakkiṉāl vipattukkaḷ tavirkka iyalātaṉa vākiviṭum.”

1541. “cilar cila kāriyatti kāriyamākak karutic ceyvatilli. viḷamparattukkākavē ceykiṉṟaṉar.”

1542. “āṟṟoḻukkup pōla-paḻakkaṅkaḷi naṭimuṟip paṭuttiṉāl makkaḷ teḷivaṭivar.”

1543. “lāpanōkkum maṉita ulakatti miruka mākki viṭṭatu.”

1544. “ceyya vēṇṭiya vēlikaḷ kaṇṇukku etirē illi tēṭip piṭittuē ceyyavēṇṭum.”

1545. “uṭal-uyirip puttiramāka vittiruppatupōla kaṭamikaḷip pattiramāka-kavaṉamāka ceypavarkaḷ nalla paṇiyāḷarkaḷ.”

1546. “paḻakkamākiviṭa vēṇṭiyavi kūṭa putiyaṉavāka iruppatiṉ kāraṇam īṭupāṭiṉmiyē”

1547. “iṉṟiya maṉitaṉi tōlurittup pārttāl oru acal kāṭṭu mirukamākavē kāṭci aḷippāṉ.”

1548. “talimi” eṉpatu iṉṟiya varalāṟṟil takutiyaṟṟatu eṉṟa poruḷ payakkum collāka urumāṟiyuḷḷa nili orntu uṇarattakkatu”

1549. “vaḻikāṭṭutalum-vaḻinaṭattutalum tāṉ talimikkuriyap paṇpukaḷ.”

1550. “ciṟanta āḷumi vāyilākat talimikku varutal iyalum”.

1551. “nammuṭiya āṟṟal-nammic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṉ āṟṟal-ākiya aṉittum payaṉpaṭumāṟu peṟṟa niliyē āḷumi nili”

1552. “varukip pativēṭṭiṉ mūlamē varuvāy varum vāḻkkiyip palar virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

1553. “uṭaliṉ nalattiṟkē uṭi; āṉāl iṉṟiya nili... atuvalla.”

1554. “ovvoru kirāmattilum celvam kuvintu kiṭakkiṟatu. uḻippāl veḷik koṇarvārit tāṉ kāṇōm.”

1555. “eṅkum vēli niṟiya irukkiṟatu. āṉāl ceyvōrik kāṇōm; āṉālum vēli tēṭi alipavarkaḷum irukkiṟārkaḷ.”

1556. “iyaṟki-āṇ-peṇ uṇarvukaḷil vēṟupāṭṭip paṭikkātapōtu; ēṉ āṇukku oru nīti-peṇṇukku oru nīti?”

1557. “āṇ maṟumaṇam ceyyalām-peṇ maṟumaṇam ceyyakkūṭātu eṉpatu-iyaṟki niyatikaḷukku muraṇāṉatu.”

1558. “nitiyi nirvakippatil kaṇṭippum, kaṇṇōṭṭamum vēṇṭaṟpālaṉa.”

1559. “aṉittum iruntum, oṉṟum illāta nili-nirvākat tāḻcciyiyē paṟicāṟṟukiṟatu.”

1560. “vīṭu eppaṭi irukkiṟatu eṉpatil alla. vāḻkki-vīṭṭil uḷḷavarkaḷ eppaṭi vāḻkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatip poṟuttatēyām.”

1561. “nam talimuṟiyil-puṇṇiya pārata pūmiyil matattiṉ peyarāl iraṇṭu paṭukolikaḷ nikaḻntuviṭṭaṉa. itu veṭkappaṭavēṇṭiya ceyti.”

1562. “aruḷum teyvamē, aṭikkavum ceykiṟatu. itu tavirkka iyalātatu.”

1563. “tiṭṭamiṭṭuc ceytāl; celvam cērntuviṭum.”

1564. “paṇikaḷi nāmē ceyvatu nallatu”

1565. “kālpantu viḷiyāṭṭil palar-mikutiyāṉa uḻippillāmal-uruṇṭuvarum pantit toṭṭu uruṭṭi maṟṟavarkaḷukkut tantuviṭuvar. tām muyaṉṟu kōl pōṭa māṭṭār. atupōla paṇikaḷik kaṭatti viṭupavarkaḷ palar.”

1566. “viḷiyāṭṭil ārvattuṭaṉ īṭupaṭupavarkaḷ pantik kēṭṭu vāṅkuvārkaḷ.”

1567. “terinta oru ceytiyiyum paṇiyiyum tiṭṭamiṭṭup payaṉuṟac ceyyāmi iḻukku.”

1568. “palanāḷ paḻakiṉālē, paḻakkam uṟuti peṟum.”

1569. “kālkaḷ iruntum kālāl naṭakkum turattukkukkūṭa ūrti tēṭi alipavaṉ cōmpēṟi.”

1570. “vāḻkkiyil ki vakikkiṉṟa paṅku aḷappila.”

1571. “palar vivātikkavē virumpukiṉṟaṉar. kalantu pēca yārum muṉvaruvatilli”.

1572. “cumattap peṟum kuṟṟaccāṭṭukkup patil collāmal tirumpa kuṟṟccāṭṭu vippatu kuṟṟam ceyyavilli eṉṟa karutti uruvākkātu.”

1573. “vaḷarcciyaṭiyātavarkaḷ tāṉ vitta atikārattil mūḻkiviṭuvar.”

1574. “kaḻivup poruḷkaḷ palanūṟu poruḷ matippuṭiyaṉa.”

1575. “vēli ceyyat terintavarkaḷ vēlikkārarkaḷāki viṭuvatilli. vēliyip payaṉuṟac ceyvatiṉ mūlamēyām.”

1576. “oṉṟip poṟuppuṇarvuṭaṉ īṭupāṭṭuṇarvuṭaṉum vāṅkik koḷpavar palariṉ aṉpip peṟuvar.”

1577. “tīyavarkaḷ cuṟucuṟuppākavum putticālikaḷākavum iruppar. ivarkaḷi muyaṉṟu māṟṟiviṭṭāl payaṉpaṭuvar.”

1578. “kāraṇam, kāriyaṅkaḷik kaṭantatē uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu.”

1579. “aṉpu kāṭṭutal eṉpatu iyalpāṉa uyirkku kuṇamākiviṭavēṇṭum.”

1580. “uḻittal eṉpatum iyalpāṉa uyirk kuṇamāṉālē vāḻa iyalum”.

1581. “enta oru naṉmiyum kūṭa tiṇittāl maṉitarkaḷ ēṟkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1582. “oruvar taṉṉuṭiya ceyaliṉmi karuti nontukoḷḷāmal cuṭṭik kāṭṭupavarmītu vētaṉiyik kāṭṭutal tiruntamāṭṭēṉ. eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷam.”

1583. “miraṭṭupavarkaḷukkup paṇivatu cāvataṟkuc camam.”

1584. “maṉitakulam vāḻat terintu koṇṭāl paruva kālamum ēval ceyyum.”

1585. “eṇṇi tiṭṭamiṭṭu kāriyaṅkaḷ ceyyātu pōṉāl kāriyak kēṭukaḷ nikaḻvatit tavirkka iyalātu.”

1586. “kuṟikaḷ ērāḷam māṟṟum muyaṟciyit tāṉ, kāṇōm.”

1587. “intiya nirvāka amippu, vaḻakkamākac celvatu. ataṟkumēl uyirppu illi.”

1588. “maṉitaṉ-vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷi aṟintu vāḻat toṭaṅkum nāḷē avaṉ uṇmiyil piṟanta nāḷ.”

1589. “āṉmīkam eṉpatu āṉmāviṉ-uyiriṉ mutirccikkuriya muyaṟcikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷum vāḻkkiyik kuṟikkum.”

1590. “paḻuttapaḻam ellārkkum cuviyāka iruppatupōla āṉmīkattil mutircci aṭintavarkaḷ yār māṭṭum aṉpu ceyvar.”

1591. “matam kaṭavuḷukkāka ēṟpaṭṭatalla-maṉitaṉukkākavē.”

1592. “tīyavarkaḷiṉ karuviyāka matam payaṉpaṭuttap peṟumpoḻutu - matam paḻippukku āḷākiṟatu.”

1593. “cātāraṇa makkaḷukkut toṇṭu ceyvatu or āṉma tiruptiyē-āṉāl vaḷarcciyum ciṟappum kiṭikkātu.”

ta-11

1594. “intiya camūka vāḻkkiyil-vitikac caṭaṅkukaḷukku uḷḷa celvākku kuṟiyavilli.”

1595. “kiṟistavarkaḷip pōla aṇi aṇi yāka ārvattuṭaṉ arppaṇippu uṇarvuṭaṉ paṇi ceyvōr kiṭittālē vaḷaralām.”

1596. “naṉṟi-vicuvācam collāl kāṭṭap peṟuvatu alla; ceyaṟpāṭukaḷiṉ mūlam kāṭṭap peṟuvatu.”

1597. “oḻuṅkamivuṭiya vāḻkkikkup pōrāṭātavarkaḷ-veṟṟi poruntiya vāḻkkiyip peṟa muṭiyātu.”

1598. “cāmārttiyacāliyāṉa oruvaṉ taṉakku carippaṭṭu varātati ellām ulakattiṟkē carippaṭṭu varātu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭuvāṉ.”

1599. “iṉṟu, kūṭṭuṟavu eṉpatu cātāraṇa paṇapparimāṟṟam maṭṭumē ceytuvarukiṟatu. itu tavaṟu. itaṉuṭaṉ āḷumi, paṇpāṭu ākiya parimāṟṟaṅkaḷukkum-vaḷarccikkum kūṭṭuṟavu payaṉpaṭum. payaṉpaṭumpaṭi iyakkavēṇṭum.”

1600. “palar, kūṭit toḻil ceyvatu eṉṟāl orē vēliyi palarceyvatu eṉpatalla. avaravarkkuḷḷa poṟuppukaḷi otticintu ceyvatu eṉpatu karuttu.”





1601. “muṟippaṭi ceyyāta nalla kāriyamkūṭa muḻup payaṉit tarātupōkum.”

1602. “palar nammuṭiya nōkkattiṟku icintu varāmal; āṉāl namatu paṭakilēyē payaṇam ceykiṉṟaṉar. itu oru vicittiramāṉa nili.”

1603. “celvam iruppatum tītu; iṉmiyum tītu-ataṉāl celvam tēṭip peṟuvatākavum-tēvikkut tēṭuvatākavum iruttalvēṇṭum.”

1604. “kālattavaṉi nirṇayam illāta etuvum payaṉ tarātu - muṭiyavum muṭiyātu.”

1605. “viḻuntu - viḻuntu, tañcāvūr pommi eḻuntirukkalām. āṉāl, maṉitaṉ kūṭātu.”

1606. “intiya nāṭṭil araciyali kuṭumpac cottāka ākkum ārvam vaḷarntu varukiṟatu; itu nallatalla.”

1607. “vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṭiyaṉavaṟṟil iṉpam tuyttu makiḻum maṉita ulakam, ēṉ koḷki vēṟupāṭukaḷiyum, appaṭik karutavilli.”

1608. “iyaṟkiyiṉ iyakkamē oru kūṭṭamippuḷḷa iyakkam.”

1609. “nilattāl maṉitaṉukkup perumi illi; maṉitaṉāl nilattukkup perumi.”

1610. “araciyal kaṭcikaḷ, āṭciyamippil ūṭuruvic ceyaṟpaṭum variyil lañcam oḻiyātu.”

1611. “cārpukaḷ vaḻippaṭṭa aṉpu, varavēṟkat takkatalla.”

1612. “teruvit tūymiyāka vittuk koḷḷātār, “tēciyam” paṟṟi vāy kiḻiyap pēcukiṉṟaṉar.”
