1613. “enta oṉṟum vaḻivaḻit taṭattil ceṉṟāl naṉmi viḷiyātu.”

1614. “māṟṟaṅkaḷ, uṟavi, vaḷarcciyip pātikkātu.”

1615. “naṉmiyilum vaḷarcciyuṇṭu.”

1616. “vaḷarcciyillāta paṇpukaḷ kūṭak kālap pōkkil taram kuṟiyum.”

1617. “paṇikaḷi, etirpārttut toṭaṅkiṉāl ciṟappāka naṭakkum.”

1618. “oṟṟumi, tēviyiṉ aṭippaṭiyil tōṉṟiyatu.”

1619. “orumippāṭu eṉpatu vēṟṟumikaḷik kaṭanta oru potuvāṉa uṟavu.”

1620. “oṉṟutal, kalattal, - itu kūṭāta oṉṟu.”

1621. “taṉittaṉmi” vaṟaṭcittaṉima uṭiyatu.”

1622. “vāḻkkiyiṉ aṭippaṭi nōkkattil tāṉ orumippāṭu kāl koḷḷa iyalum.”

1623. “potumakkaḷ palvēṟu kuṭimippaṇpukaḷil vaḷartal avaciyam.”

1624. “kaṭavuḷ nampikkiyē pōtumāṉatu. mataṅkaḷ vēṇṭiyatilli.”

1625. “eṉṉuṭiya pakivaṉum palamāka iruntu nāṉ mōtiṉāltāṉ eṉ palam vaḷarum.”

1626. “kuṟinta aḷavu vākkāḷarkaḷi nāṉ cantittu vākkup peṟa piracāram ēṉ? ellām veḷiccamtāṉ!”

1627. “patavikaḷukku ēṟpaṭum nerukkaṭi, paṇikaḷukku ēṟpaṭṭāl nallatu.”

1628. “cariyākac ceyyātār, ceyyāmikkut tām nōkum iyalpu varāta variyil tiruttam varātu.”

1629. “aluvalaka naṭimuṟikaḷ, paṭippiṉikaḷ vaḻi vantaviyē! ivi piṉpaṟṟappaṭātu pōṉāl tōlviyē varum.”

1630. “kaṇap poḻutum māṟum ulakiyaliṉ māṟṟaṅkaḷ tavirkka iyalātaṉa.”

1631. “aracu, vīṭṭu vācaṟ paṭikku vantālum viḻippillātavar palar uṇṭu.”

1632. “iṉṟiya maṉitar palar tēvikku āṭpaṭṭu ācippaṭuvativiṭa aḻukkāṟṟukku āṭpaṭṭē ācippaṭukiṉṟaṉar.”

1633. “paḻakum paṇpu aṟiyātavarkaḷ mēlatikārikaḷāka vantu viṭṭāl avarkaḷōṭu otticintu paṇi ceytal aritu.”

1634. “araciyal vātikaḷ taṅkaḷuṭiya viruppaṅkaḷi maṟṟavarkaḷ mītu ēṟṟuvar.”

1635. “uḻippil payaṉpaṭuttap peṟāta āṟṟal, nīriḻivu nōyākiṟatu.”

1636. “cintittut tiṭṭamiṭṭāl ceṉṟa kālattil ceyyappaṭāmal viṭupaṭṭup pōṉavi evvaḷavu eṉpatu teriyavarum.”

1637. “ācikaḷ aḷavukku muyaṟci illi.”

1638. “cutantaram kaṟṟuk koṭuttirukkum orē kāriyam maṉukkaḷ eḻutuvatutāṉ.”

1639. “kirāmaṅkaḷ taṉṉiṟivuṭiya kuṭiyaracāka viḷaṅkavēṇṭum.”

1640. “ceytavaṟṟic caripārttal eṉpatu tavirkka muṭiyātatu."

1641. “araciyalil uḷḷa ārvam ceyaṟpāṭukaḷil illiyē!”

1642. “pacumikku īṭu etuvumilli.”

1643. “namatu nāṭṭiṉ araciyal potu vāḻkki kolkuṟumpu ceyyum kayavarkaḷ kikkup pōyk koṇṭirukkiṟatu.”

1644. “anītikaḷik kaṇṭikkattakātavarkaḷ anītikaḷukku uṭaṉpaṭṭavarkaḷ tām.”

1645. “nām meḷḷa meḷḷa paḻakka vacattāl tīmikaḷukku uṭaṉpaṭṭuviṭukiṉṟōm.”

1646. “kamyūṉisṭukaḷ uriyavakiyil intiya nāṭṭukkuc cēvi ceyyavilli.”

1647. “intiya kamyūṉisṭukaḷ peyarāltāṉ. uṇmiyāṉa kamyūṉisṭṭukaḷ alla.”

1648. “enta niliyilum yātoṉṟiyum viṭṭuk koṭukka muṉvarātavarkaḷ eṇṇikki vaḷarntu varukiṟatu.”

1649. “celavukkup paṇam tēṭutal, paṇattukkuc celavu tēṭutal; tēvikkup paṇam tēṭutal.”

1650. “mēṟṟici nāṭukaḷil camūka nākarikam vaḷarntuḷḷatu. āṉāl intiyāvil “camūkamē’ iṉṉum uruvākavilli.”

1651. “utavi ceytal eṉṟa aṟam, aṟaṅkaḷil ellām ciṟanta aṟam.”

1652. “uṭkuḻukkaḷ illāta amippukkaḷāl tāṉ, nāṭṭukku nallaṉa ceyyamuṭiyum.”

1653. “uṭal iyaṅkukiṟatu; āṉāl nām iyaṅkuvatilli.”

1654. “ētāvatu oru āvēcam vantālaṉṟi paṇikaḷiṉ toṭakkam varātu.”

1655. “nāmē ceyvatu-naṟpayaṉ tarum.”

1656. “metuvākac ceytālum-toṭarcci iruntāl payaṉ kiṭikkum.”

1657. “tiṭṭattukkāka vāḻkkiyalla - vāḻkkikkākavē tiṭṭam uruvākiṟatu.”

1658. “ārpparavam makkaḷi īrppatāl-eṅkum ārpparavam naṭipeṟukiṟatu.

1659. “araciyalil putumukam kāṇpataritu.”

1660. “uṉṉip pukaḻkiṟavarkaḷ-uṉakkuk kēṭu ceykiṟārkaḷ.”

1661. “kalveṭṭukaḷ mūlam niṉivip parāmarippu eṉpatu-avarkaḷiṭam uṇmi illi eṉpataṉ aṭiyāḷam.”

1662. “etirmaṟi iyakkaṅkaḷ aḻikkum; ākkam ceyyā?”

1663. “etirmaṟi iyakkaṅkaḷ etirppukkuriyaṉa maṟintuviṭṭāl oru cūṉyatti uruvākkum.”

1664. “enta oru naṉmiyum tīmiyum oru nāḷil, oru poḻutil tōṉṟuvaṉa alla.”

1665. “vāḻkkiyi viyāpāramāka aṉumatittal taṟkoliyākum.”

1666. “cākki naṉittut tūkkik koṇṭu pōtali okkum, aṟintum tuṉpattic cumattal."

1667. “oru tamiḻaṉ ñāṉiyāka viḷaṅkutali pārppaṉar matat talivarkaḷ ēṟkamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1668. “cātāraṇa vikkōl kūṭa oru makiḻvuntup payaṇattit taṭuttuviṭukiṟatu. ātalāl kēṭṭiṉic ceyya ciṟiyōrālum iyalum.”

1669. “araciyal kaṭci ūrvalatti, kaṭamiyi viṭa uyarntatākak karutupavarkaḷāl araciyalukkum payaṉ illi; kaṭami ulakattukkum payaṉ illi.”

1670. “tūṅkutalum uḻippāḷikaḷukku oru vitak kaṭamiyēyākum.”

1671. “cikkalē illāta iṭattilum cilar viti muṟiyi mīṟukiṉṟaṉar. ēṉ, paḻakkattiṟku aṭimippaṭṭa nili.”

1672. “ellōrukkum ellāvaṟṟilum īṭupaṭutal payaṉaṟṟavikaḷiyē vaḷarkkum.”

1673. “muyaṟciyiṉ vāyilil mutal aṭi kūṭa eṭuttu vikkātār muṭiyavilli eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ēṟka iyalāta oṉṟu.”

1674. “kalviyil ārvam uṭiyār kūḻukkum kañcikkum veṭkap paṭamāṭṭār.”

1675. “paṭittavarkaḷiṭam kūṭa camūka vāḻnili uṇarvukaḷ kālkoḷḷavilli.”

1676. “namatu nāṭṭil araciyalil īṭupaṭṭiruppavarkaḷil pātippēr patavikaḷ - perumikkē! araciyal naṭatta alla."

1677. “cātāraṇa makkaḷiṉ āṉmāvi viḻitteḻac ceytāltāṉ vaḷarccip paṇikaḷ payaṉ tarum.”

1678. “poṟuppaṟṟa nili eṉpatu namatu nāṭṭukkum paḻakkamākiviṭṭatu.”

1679. “kāraṇa, kāriyaṅkaḷi kaṭantatatē uṇmiyāṉa aṉpu.”

1680. “tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ araciyal iyakkaṅkaḷ-oḷitalam tara iyalātavi.”

1681. “oru maṉitar evvaḷavu ciṉṉavarāka iruntālum avar aḷavukku akanti-aḻukkāṟu koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.”

1682. “tamiḻ nāṭṭil kiṟittuvarkaḷ-iṉṟum vāḻkkiyil intukkaḷē.”

1683. “ilañcam eṉpatu iṉṟiya paḻakkaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭatu.”

1684. “kāla uṇarvu naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷukku ellām tāy.”

1685. “paḻakap paḻakattāṉ entap paḻakkamum uṟutipeṟum.”

1686. "camayat talivarkaḷukku, celvantarkaḷiṭam paḻakkam vantuviṭṭālē, aṟaneṟip pōtaṉikaḷ ticimāṟiviṭum.”

1687. “iṉṟiya aṟaneṟikaḷ - caṭṭaṅkaḷ aṉittum ēḻikaḷukku etirāṉaviyē.”

1688. “maṅkiyar palar aṇikalaṉkaḷi-aḻakukkāka aṇivatilli - maṟṟavarkaḷukkuk kāṭṭavēyām.”

1689. “ōrāṇṭu kaṭantuviṭṭatu, eṉṟu ūtiya uyarvu etirpārppavarkaḷ, orāṇṭil naṭantatu eṉṉa eṉṟu kaṇakkup pōṭṭup pārkka maṟukkiṟārkaḷ.”

1690. “ellōrumē veṟṟi kiṭikkum eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. appaṭiyāṉāl tōṟpatu yār?”

1691. “veṟṟi, tōlvi maṉitarkaḷiṉ taratti nirṇayippatu alla. veṟṟi, tōlvi avarkaḷi eppaṭi ākkukiṟatu eṉpati poṟuttuttāṉ!”

1692. “pala nāḷkaḷ cintittut tokutta ātāraṅkaḷ aṭippaṭiyiltāṉ oru āyvukkūṭṭam naṭatta iyalum.”

1693. “teḷivāṉa viparaṅkaḷ, tiṭṭaṅkaḷ illāta ceyaṟpāṭu araṅkiṉṟi āṭuvati okkum.”

1694. “taṉṉuṭiya vacatikaḷukku ēṟpa, kālattip payaṉpaṭuttupavarkaḷ, kaṭamikaḷic ceypavarkaḷ orupoḻutum maṟṟavarkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1695. “oruṅkiṇippu mikka uṇarvu illāta niṟuvaṉam ceyaṟpāṭṭil payaṉ kiṭikkātu. iḻappum ēṟpaṭum.”

1696. “etiyum kālak keṭuvuṭaṉ ceytu paḻakātār nāṭkaḷi iḻantuviṭuvar.”

1697. “ceyal māṭṭātāriṭam kōṭi koṭut teṉṉa? yātoru payaṉum irā!”

1698. “nōkkattil oṉṟātārip paṇiyil uṭaṉuḻippāḷarkaḷāka ēṟpatu kaṭiṉam.”

1699. “paṇam pattum ceyyum eṉpati nirūpaṇam ceyvatē varalāṟu.”

1700. “intiyā oru nāṭṭu” uṇmiyēyāyiṉum iṉṟiruppatu pōl oru nāṭu amippu irukkak kūṭātu.”

oru nāṭu

orē mānilam āṭci ceykiṟatu;

orē moḻi āṭci ceykiṟatu;

orē matam āṭci ceykiṟatu.

orē kuṭumpam āṭci ceykiṟatu.





1701. “cāmārttiyam vantamiyum aḷavukku vāymi vantu poruntuvatilli.”

1702. “enta oru paṇiyum aṭittaḷa makkaḷiṉ mēmpāṭṭukku utavāviṭil atu paṇiyalla; oru vakiyāṉa vaṇikamēyākum.”

1703. “cuya viruppattāl ceyyap peṟum paṇiyē aṟam cārnta paṇi.”

1704. “uyar kuṟikkōḷukku urimiyākkik koḷḷātōr - taṭṭaccu iyantiram pōlap paṇi ceypavar. payaṉ irātu.”

1705. “uyar kuṟikkōḷ ilātār ciṟappuṟum paṇikaḷi ceyyār; atē pōḻtu piḻikaḷum ceyvar.”

1706. “uḷ vaḷarcciyum atāvatu āṉmāvum vaḷarntāltāṉ - kalvi - kēḷvi payaṉuṟum.”

1707. “oruvaruṭiya kaṇkaḷ - kātukaḷ aṟivārnta niliyil cumūka uṇarvil iyaṅkiṉālēyē ērāḷamāṉa varalāṟṟuṇmikaḷ kiṭikkum. ceyaṟpāṭṭukkuriya untu caktikaḷum kiṭikkum.”

1708. “taṉṉalam eṉpatu pēyiṉum koṭumiyāṉatu.”

1709. “piṟar nalam pēṇiyatāl keṭṭār yārum illi”.

1710. “intiyāvil makkaḷāṭci muṟi illi kuḻukkaḷiṉ āṭcimuṟiyē uḷḷatu.”

1711. “aracāṅka iyantiram - oru carakkuntu vaṇṭi, ataṉ uyirppuḷḷa ceyaṟpāṭu irukkātu.”

1712. “cilari eḻuppavē iyalavilli; avarkaḷi nontu kālaṅkaṭattuvativiṭa eṭuttuc ceyvatu nallatu.”

1713. “muyaṟciyuṭiya vāḻkkiyil varum iṭarkaḷ-veṟṟiyit taṭuttuviṭātu.”

1714. “iṭarkaḷ vēṟu - iyalāmi eṉpatu vēṟu.”

1715. iṭarkaḷiyē iyalāmi karutukiṟavarkaḷāl-kāriya cātaṉi ceyya iyalātu.”

1716. “celvam peṟutal peritalla-pēṉutalē ariya kaṭami.”

1717. “vāykkum kālattiṟkuriya paṇiyituveṉa nirṇayittu ceyka, ippaṭic ceyka, ippaṭic ceytāl paṇikaḷiṉ cumi kuṟiyum; kālam miñcum oru pōtum nerukka varātu.”

1718. “āciriyarkaḷukku paḷḷiyē tirukkōyil: māṇavarkaḷē vikrakaṅkaḷ; aṟivē nivētaṉam.”

1719. “ariya kaṭamikaḷic ceypavarkaḷiviṭa cōmpēṟikku poḻutu eḷitil pōyviṭum. avarkaḷukku poḻutu pōvatu teriyātatāl.”

1720. “orunāṭṭumarattil uyariya iṉatti oṭṭuppōṭṭu uyariya paḻamaram eḷitil uruvākka muṭikiṟatu. āṉāl eḷitil maṉitarkaḷip paṭikka muṭivatilli.”

1721. “oṉṟi oṉṟāl īṭuceyvatu eḷitu. palavāka aṉumatittāl cumiyākiviṭum.”

1722. “kiyālākātavarkaḷ - kālaṅkaṭattupavarkaḷāl, piraccaṉikaḷukkut tīrvu kāṉa iyalātu.”

1723. “ceyaltiṟaṉ eṉpatu tāy. ceyalpaṭutalē tanti.”

1724. “uḻippum - nukarvum vāḻkkiyi iyakkum iraṇṭu uruḷikaḷ.”

1725. “camūka anītikaḷ - āṭciyilamintu viṭṭāl makkaḷukku vimōcaṉam illi.”

1726. “intiya nāṭṭiṉ ēḻikaḷ - aṟivum teḷivum illātavarkaḷ, pōrāḷiyarkaḷum,alla; aṟivāḷikaḷ cuyanalavātikaḷ - cōmpēṟikaḷ; araciyalvātikaḷ atikārap paciyiṉar; celvantarkaḷ nīriḻivu nōyiṉar ātalāl puraṭci taṭip paṭukiṟatu.”

1727. “intiya maṇṇil potu uṭimi malara neṭiya nāḷ piṭikkum.”

1728. “intiyāvil karutturuvam koṭukkum karuvikaḷ valimiyākavilli."

1729. “kaṭamikaḷiṉ vaḻi urimi peṟuvatu - kaṟpiṉ vaḻi makappēṟu peṟṟār pōla.”

1730. “karuppuppaṇam eṉpatu - karṇaṉ piṟantatu pōla - vāḻntatu pōla:”

1731. “vaḷarccikkuriya uṇarvukaḷi nāḷ tōṟum peṟṟu vaḷarntāltāṉ - veṟṟikaḷ kiṭikkum.”

1732. “vāḻkkiyil tēkkam kūṭavē kūṭātu.”

1733. “palar vēli ceyvatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl kaṇakkup pārttāl nalla viṭi kiṭippa tilli. cariyāṉa nērattil-cariyāṉa muṟiyil cariyāṉa aḷavukkuc ceyaṟpaṭātatē kāraṇam.”

1734. “namatu aracu iyantiram perumpālum paḻutākiviṭṭatu.”

1735. “iṉṟu aracu iyantiratti iyakkuvatu ilaṭciyaṅkaḷ alla-ilaṭcaṅkaḷ.”

1736. “nallavaṉ aṟivurikku āṭpaṭṭuc ceyvāṉ. keṭṭavaṉ taṇṭaṉikku āṭpaṭṭu ceyvāṉ.”

1737. “namatu nāṭṭiṉ varappukkaḷilē āyirak kaṇakkāṉa ēkkarkaḷ.”

1738. “cellāk kāci yārum virumpuvatilli. āṉāl, celvam perukap payaṉpaṭāta-celāvaṇiyillāta vāḻvi virumpukiṉṟaṉar.”

1739. “uḻikkātavaṉ vāḻkki cellāk kācu.”

1740. “taṉṉalat tīṅkillāta cilar ciṟiya piṟar nalap paṇikaḷik kūṭac ceyvatilli. anta aḷavukku camūka uṇarvu illi.”

1741. “tiriyamāka nāṭkuṟippu eḻutik kāṭṭi ūtiyam vāṅkupavarkaḷ vēcikaḷilum koṭiyar.”

1742. “ceyta paṇiyiṉ aḷavu kaṭuku, kāla tāmatam mikuti. payaṉō etirmaṟi. ippaṭi vēli pārttāl eppaṭi vaḷarcci tōṉṟum.”

1743. “taṉakkut toṭarpillāta ceytiyi aṟiya virumputal ati maṟṟavarkaḷiṭam kūṟutal ākiyaṉa mōcamāṉa maṉitarkaḷiṉ ceyaṟpāṭu.”

1744. “cevvi pārkkat teriyātavar-oṉṟu mūrkkar; allatu cuyanalavātikaḷ.”

1745. “orumutal vaḷarntāltāṉ vaḷarcci.”

1746. “oṉṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟākat toṭarcciyākak kāl vittu naṭappatu naṭi. oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka ceyaṟpaṭuvatu naṭi-oḻukkam.”

1747. “muṭṭiyiṭum vāttik koṉṟatu, aṉṟu kati; iṉṟu yatārattamāṉa vāḻkki.”

1748. “makkaḷ maṟappatil vallavarkaḷ eṉṟa nampikkiyuṭaṉtāṉ araciyalvātikaḷ pēcukiṉṟaṉar.”

1749. “oru paṇiyi aṟivārnta āyvuṭaṉum muḻumiyāṉa payaṉpāṭṭu nōkkuṭaṉum ceytāl muḻumiyāṉa payaṉ viḷiyum.”

1750. “kāla tāmataṅkaḷ pala iḻappukkaḷit tantuḷḷaṉa.”

1751. āriyat tiṉi akaṟṟi anta iṭattil tamiḻi vikkavēṇṭum-appaṭiyallātu āriyam pōṉṟa oṉṟi tamiḻil ceytu vippatil eṉṉa payaṉ.” .

1752. “aṉpu-pakti-toṇṭu-paṇi ākiya aṉittukkum avvapoḻutu kaṇakku muṭittu-kaṇakku pārttu putukkaṇakku pōṭavēṇṭum.

1753. “iṟivaṉuṭiya tiruvaruḷ niṟinta āṟṟalkaḷ; kampikaḷilum-kayiṟukaḷilumā cellum”

1754. caṭaṅkukaḷil mīṇṭum vīḻtal kūṭātu.”

1755. “aṉṟu oru cūrapatumaṉ; iṉṟō āyiram āyiram cūrapatumaṉkaḷ.”

1756. “nayampaṭap pēciṉāl maṭṭum pōtātu - cūtillāmal iraṇṭu poruḷ paṭāmal pēca vēṇṭum.”

1757. “eṉ matam uyarntatu.” eṉṟu karutupavarkaḷ, matac caṇṭiyi vilikoṭuttu vāṅkukiṟārkaḷ.”

1758. “kēṭkāmal pēcukiṟavarkaḷiṭam cikkik koṇṭāl meḷaṉamāka iruppatē vaḻi.”

1759. “iṉpattil tuṉpak kalappillātatē iṉpam-antamil iṉpam-cutta iṉpam. cutta iṉpam.

1760. pirārattuvatti (nukarum viṉi) maṉa niṟivuṭaṉum, makiḻvuṭaṉum aṉupavittāl pukiccal irukkātu-puḻukkaṅkaḷ irukkātu.”

1761. “neruppiṉuṭiya eriyum aḷavukku viñciṭa, ākutip poruḷip pōṭṭāl vēḷvit tī erivatilli-peruntīṉik kāraṉiṉ āṉmā eḻucciyaṭivatilli.”

1762. “cōṣalicam vaḷarum nāṭṭil, kalvi kūṭṭuṟavu toḻil ākiyaviyē mutaṉmiyuṭiyaṉa namatu nāṭṭilē varuvāy tuṟiyum (āṭci), kāval tuṟiyum paṉmaṭaṅku vaḷarntuḷḷatu. pōlīcu irājyam pōl terikiṟatu.”

1763. “potumakkaḷukku vēlikkum, ataṟkēṟṟa kūlikkumē uttaravātam vēṇṭum; cōṟṟukkum tuṇikkum alla.”

1764. “muṉṉēṟa muṭiyum, muṉṉēṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarvē ēḻi makkaḷiṭam illi! viti paṟṟiya nampikki āḻamākap patintuḷḷatu.”

1765. “nirvākam periyatāka vaḷara vaḷara anta amippil uḷḷavarkaḷiṭam poṟuppuṇarvu illātu pōṉāl vaḷarcci pātikkum. ūtiya palūṉ pōla veṭittuviṭum.”

1766. “evvaḷavutāṉ nerukkamāṉa paḻakkam iruntālum iṭiyil paṇam pukuntu viḷiyāṭiṉāl maṉitarkaḷ keṭṭup pōvārkaḷ.”

1767. “paṇattāciyāl paṇpāṭiḻappavarkaḷ paṇam kāliyāṉapiṟaku tēṭuvāriṉṟi alivārkaḷ.”

1768. “enta oru paṇiyiyum toṭakka niliyilēyē eḷimiyākak karutiviṭāmal kaṭumiyāṉatākak karuti niṟinta muyaṟciyi mēṟkoḷvatu kāriya cātaṉikku vaḻi.”

1769. “kāla uṇarvu naṟpaḻakkaṅkaḷukku ellām tāy.”

1770. “oru periya nīḷamāṉa tērccaṅkiliyil-iṭiyil oru kampi muṟintālum tērccaṅkili

ta-12 payaṉaṟṟatākiviṭukiṟatu. oru nirvākattil oruvar palavīṉarāka iruntālum pātikkum.”

1771. “tēr iḻuviyiṉ poḻutu-tērvaṭattil oru pakuti aṟuntupōṉālum āyirak kaṇakkāṉavarkaḷ kīḻē vīḻntuviṭuvar. atupōla virintu vaḷarum nirvākattil oruvar poṟup pillātu naṭantu koṇṭālum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa pēriṉ vāḻkkiyip pātikkum.”

1772. “kaṇakkup pārkkumpoḻutu vēliyiṉ aḷavum, payaṉum cariyāka illātu pōṉāl vēli ceytatākak kūṟa iyalātu.”

1773. “vēliyiṉ payaṉi aṉupavikka iyalāta niliyil vāyiṉāl vēli pārttatākak kūṟuvatu ēṟpuṭiyataṉṟu.”

1774. “toṭutal, toṭartal, muṭittal iṭiyīṭu illāta paṇinili uṭiyaṉa.”

1775. “nāḷtōṟum, celva varavum, celva vaḷarccikkuriya vāyilkaḷum kaṇkāṇikkap peṟutal vēṇṭum.”

1776. “varavukaḷ kaṇkāṇikkap peṟutalē celvap pātukāppukku uriya vaḻi.”

1777. “mātavi, maṇimēkaliyi, tuṟavu neṟiyil āṟṟuppaṭuttiyatu, kōvalaṉiṉ pukaḻip pātukāppataṟkākavē!”

1778. “mātavi, maṇimēkaliyit tuṟavu neṟiyil āṟṟuppaṭuttiyatu. kōvalaṉiṭam aṭinta ēmāṟṟattiṉ viḷivē!"

1779. “naṭpu-kātal arppaṇipput taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa. ataṉāl, taṉṉalam pēṇutal irukkātu.”

1780. “ti.mu.kaḻakattiṉar, taṅkaḷuṭiya cuvaroṭṭiyil em.ji.āri veṟṟipeṟac ceytu viṭṭaṉar.”

1781. “nakarattāriṭam peṇmiyip pēṇum iyalpu uṇṭu.”

1782. “kuṟivāṉa ilakku nirṇayippatu uḷḷatiyum keṭuttuviṭum.”

1783. “perum poṟuppukkaḷil iruppavarkaḷ eṇṇikkiyil “oruvar” āṉālum camūkattiṉ matippīṭu tarutal vēṇṭum.”

1784. “viḷamparam eṉpatu virumpattakka oṉṟu alla.”

1785. “aracāṅkam kuṭimakkaḷ niliyilum, taṉippaṭṭa muṟiyilum, camutāya niliyilum ceyya iyalātaṉavaṟṟic ceytal vēṇṭum.”

1786. “vāḻkkikku uttaravātam vaḻaṅkāta aracukku taṇṭaṉi tara urimiyilli.”

1787. “muṉṉaṇiyil niṟpatu eṉpatu muntirik koṭṭi mātiri niṟpatākātu. muntirikkoṭṭi mātiri niṟpatu muṉṉaṇiyil iruppatāka ākātu.”

1788. “cuvimikka paruppi uḷḷiṭāka uṭiya muntirik koṭṭi, paḻattiṉ cuvi uṭaṉpaṭāmiyāl paḻattiṉiṉṟu pirintu veḷiyē vantuviṭṭatu. āṉālum piṟanta pāvattiṟkāka uṟavu niliyil oṭṭik koṇṭirukkiṟatu.”

1789. “camūkatti aṉittu vaḷarttal kaṭumiyāṉa paṇi. intap paṇikku kōpamē ākātu; kūṭātu.”

1790. “oruvar taṉ vāḻkkiyil pātikkap paṭumpoḻutu araṇ tēṭuvar. āṉāl avarkaḷ pātikkumpaṭi kāriyaṅkaḷic ceyvār.”

1791. “aracukaḷiṉ atikāraṅkaḷiyum viṭak kūṭutalāṉa atikāram peṟṟatu camūkattiṉ maṉap pōkkum, paḻiya paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum!”

1792. “kaṇṇakiyāl pāṇṭiya araci etirkka muṭintatu. āṉāl pūmpukār nakara makkaḷiṉ paḻakkatti māṟṟa muṭiyavilli.”

1793. “kaṇavaṉ - maṉivi caṇṭiyil kuḻantikaḷiyum kiyil koṭuttu, viraṭṭuvatu eṉṉa niyāyam?”

1794. “eṉṉa vēli ceytōm eṉpatik kūṟiyum kāṭṭiyum nirūpaṇam ceyya iyalātavarkaḷ kūliyi eṇṇik kēṭpatu eṉṉa niyāyam.”

1795. “entavitat takutiyum illāta oruvaṉ kaṇavaṉākiviṭṭāl avaṉuṭiya maṉiviyi avaṉ aṭimiyeṉak karuti naṭattum maṉappōkkiltāṉ peṇṇaṭimittaṉam irukkiṟatu.”

1796. “taṉṉuṭiya ceyalkaḷukku viḷakkam kūṟa iyalātavarkaḷ kōpappaṭuvārkaḷ.”

1797. “marumakaḷ eṉṟa uṟavu muṟiyi amittum māmiyārkaḷiṉ maṉam iṟaṅkavilli.”

1798. “oru eḷiyavaṉiṭattil kūṭa nāṉ appaṭittāṉ ceyvēṉ” eṉṟu collak kūṭātu. appaṭic coṉṉāl cāṉṟōr koṭumi eṉṟu paḻi tūṟṟuvar.”

1799. “māṉam eṉpatu kiñciṟṟum illātavarkaḷtāṉ avamāṉam paṟṟi ārpparippar.”

1800. “kāyampaṭṭa kālil kūccam iruppatāl valimiyuṭaṉ eṭuttu ūṉṟātatāl mēlum iṭipāṭu uṇṭākum. kāyampaṭṭa kālil uḷḷa poymmiyāṉa akkaṟiyāl antak kālip payaṉpaṭuttāmal vāḷāviṭuvatāl payaṉpāṭṭu nili iḻantu pōkiṟatu. paṭṭa kālilēyē paṭum.”





1801. “oruvarukkuk kēṭu vantateṉil - kēṭu vantavuṭaṉ, kēṭṭiṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu aṟivaṟinta āḷviṉiyi mēṟkoṇṭu kēṭṭiṉi nīkkik koḷḷātu vantuḷḷa kēṭṭiṟkuk kavalip paṭupavarkaḷi nōkkik kūṟiyatu” keṭṭa kuṭiyē keṭum’ eṉpatu.

1802. “maṉam keṭṭu uṟavu keṭṭuppōṉa kuṭiyē keṭum. maṟṟa kēṭukaḷāl atāvatu vaṟumiyāl piṇiyāl oru kuṭi keṭṭuviṭātu.”

1803. “ceyalaṟṟup pōṉa”-eṉpati viḷakkuvatu “paṭuṭa” eṉṟa col. uyirppu kuṟintu ceyalaṟṟup pōṉa kālilēyē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṇiyum ceyalaṟṟa taṉmiyum tōṉṟum nalla uyirppuṭaṉ ceyalum uḷḷa kālil piṇi vantālum nīṅkum. kālukku uyirppu uḻippāl varuvatu.”

1804. “piṟappiṉ kāraṇamum cūḻalum nalla vakiyiṉatāka amiyāviṭil nīṅkāt tuṉpam vantu poruntum.”

1805. “āciyil viḷivatu aṉittum avalamē.”

1806. “vaṉmamum vaḷarum taṉmiyatē. tōlvikaḷālum aṟivurikaḷālum kūṭa vaṉmamuṭiyavari māṟṟutal aritu.”

1807. “tīmiyil ellām tīmi’ vaṉ kaṇmiyē!”

1808. “iṉṟum pēccu, ellōrukkum camanili vāḻvu. naṭappatu vallāṅku vāḻvōrukku vāḻ vaḷittalē.”

1809. “iṉṟu kiyūṭṭu vāṅkupavarkaḷ oḷivu maṟivuṭaṉ vāṅkuvatilli. veḷippaṭiyākavē vikitam vitittu varipōlat taṇṭukiṉṟaṉar.

1810. “inta nūṟṟāṇṭu ceyta makattāṉa cātaṉi, kiyūṭuṭi tēciyamayappaṭuttiyatu.”

1811. “otta nōkku, otta ceyal, otta tiṟaṉ uṭiyōrāka naṭpu kiṭittal aritu.”

1812. “parivuṇarvukaḷ taṇṇīrip pōlat tāṉ, vaḷarccikkut tuṇi ceyyum. parivu uṇavāka amintu utavi ceyyātu.”

1813. “kāraṇaṅkaḷē illāmal mōtikkoḷḷum aḷavukku aṟiyāmi vaḷarntirukkiṟatu.”

1814. “paṭittavarkaḷ kūṭa kāraṇa kāriyaṅkaḷi ārāyntu piraccaṉiyi, tīrvukku vaḻi kāṇāmal vaḷarttuviṭukiṟaṉar.”

1815. “iruvēṟu aṇiyiṉar eṉṟa nili uruvāṉa piṟaku, naṭuvunili aṇi oṉṟu illātu pōṉāl vampu vaḷarum.”

1816. “im! eṉṟāl, cāti, kaṭuci aṭippaṭiyil kūṭuvatu virumpat takkatalla.”

1817. “iṉṟu makkaḷ, vākkāḷarkaḷ kuṟi, niṟi pārttu vākkaḷippatilli. avarkaḷ viruppam pōla.“

1818. “kaṭanta kālat tavaṟukaḷi makkaḷ maṟantiruppārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇiyē pala araciyal talivarkaḷ ulā varukiṟārkaḷ.”

1819. “pacciyākac colvatutāṉ palarukkum puriyum. paṇpaṭṭa moḻiyil colvatu purivatilli. oru vakiyāṉa pāmarat taṉmi.”

1820. “oru tīmiyik kaṇṭippatu maṭṭumē pōtātu. tīrvum kāṭṭa vēṇṭum.”

1821. “tīmiyuṭiya naparik kaṇṭittāl varavēṟkiṟavarkaḷ, tīmiyik kaṇṭittāl varavēṟka muṉvaruvatilli, ivarkaḷ naparip paṟṟiyē kēṭṭup paḻakiyavarkaḷ.”

1822. “muṉpē kēṭṭu paḻakiya ceytiyi maṉattil vittuk koṇṭē kalantu pēca muṉvarupavarkaḷ āyvu maṉappāṉmi illātavarkaḷ.”

1823. “uccantali urōmam mutal, uḷḷaṅ-kāl vari uyirppu nili irukkiṟatu. uḷḷaṅ kālil eṟumpu ūriṉālum uṭaṉ ceyti talikkup pōy tali kiyi aḻittu taḷḷu eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttum. atupōla oru iyakkam ataṉ kiḷikaḷilum, irukkavēṇṭum.

1824. “camūka vāḻkkiyil māṟutal kāṇa viḻiyāmal puṟattē ciṉṉaṅkaḷ amippatilēyē palar muṉikiṉṟaṉar. itu oru uṇrcci”.

1825. “iṉṟiya tamiḻarkaḷilpalar peyaraḷavil tamiḻar. ivarkaḷukku moḻi uṇarvu illi.”

1826. “araciyal iyakkaṅkaḷukku patavicukam, atikāra āci irukkum vari uruppaṭiyāṉa camūka māṟṟattiṟkup puraṭci ceyya māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1827. “pala tavaṟukaḷ, paḻakkatti māṟṟa iyalāmiyiṉālēyē yām.”

1828. “toḻil nuṭpattuṭaṉ kūṭiya uḻippē payaṉtarum”.

1829. “paṇiyārvam illāmal piḻippik karutupavarkaḷ payaṉpaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.”

1830. “yār māṭṭum kaṭumpki koṇṭu aḻikka muyaṉṟālum kēṭu varum.”

1831. “iṉṟiya ulakattil ciṉavāmal iruppataṟku muyaṉṟu, veṟṟi peṟṟu viṭṭāl atu oru cātaṉiyē!”

1832. “vitikaḷ, muṟikaḷ, neṟikaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ paṭippiṉikaḷil piṟantavi. avaṟṟi nām ēṟkātu pōṉāl muṉṉōr peṟṟa tōlvikaḷi nāmum peṟuvōm.”

1833. “perumpālum ariya cātaṉikaḷic ceyya muyaṉṟōr taṉi maṉita niliyilēyē ceytuḷḷaṉar. pantikku varuvatu pōl aṉivarum paṇik kaḷattiṟku varamāṭṭārkaḷ.”

1834. “nāḷatu variyil uyariya ilaṭucīyaṅkaḷukkākap pōrāṭiyavarkaḷ cākaṭikkap paṭṭē vantuḷḷaṉar. atil, aṉṉi intirākāntiyum oruvarākiviṭṭār.”

1835. “vētaṉi, ūkkamuṭiyōrukku paṭikkum āṟṟaliyē tarum.”

1836. “aṉittum iruntum ciṟappāka vāḻātōr koṭuttu vikkātavarkaḷ eṉṟu collit tāṉ aḻavēṇṭiyirukkiṟatu.”

1837. “varavukkuḷ celavu” eṉpatu taṟkāppu yukti. āṉāl varavukku elli kaṭṭakkūṭātu.”

1838. “nalla paṇi veḷiyē terintavuṭaṉ avaravar ūrukkuc ceyyumpaṭi kōruvārkaḷ; ataṉ aṭippaṭiyi aṟiyāmalē.”

1839. “vaṅkiyil iṭṭu vippatum iṉṟu aṟamēyākum.”

1840. “veppam niṟinta nilappakutikaḷil eṇṇeyk kuḷiyal iṉṟiyamiyātatē.”

1841. “uṇavum uḻippum aḷavākak kiṭiyā viṭil nōyēyām.”

1842. “vēliyit tēṭic ceyyāmal kiyil vanta vēliyic ceypavarkaḷāl uṭaṉuṟi eḻuttarkaḷāka iruppatu cāttiyamilli.”

1843. “enta oru paṇiyiyum oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattiṟkuḷ muṭikkum viḻivu tēvi.”

1844. “viḻittiruttal eṉpatu āyatta niliyil iruppatiyē kuṟikkum.”

1845. “eppōtum āyatta niliyil piṇippu illātavarkaḷiṭamiruntu palamaṇit tuḷikaḷ naḻuvi viṭum. kālattiyum ceyta vēliyiyum kaṉakkiṭṭup pārkka muṭiyātu."

1846. “muṟṟāka nallavarkaḷum illi; muṟṟāka keṭṭavarkaḷum illi.”

1847. “ēvavum ceykalāṉtāṉ tēṟāṉ avvuyirkku pōom aḷavum or nōy” eṉṟa tirukkuṟaḷ, vāḻviṉ paṭippiṉiyil piṟanta tirukkuṟaḷ. iṉṉum intak kuṟaḷukku ilakkiyamākap palar uḷar.”

1848. “tavaṟukaḷi uṇarttukiṟa poḻutu, etirppuṇarcci kāṭṭupavar allatu payappaṭukiṟavar, yātoru uṇarcciyum kāṭṭātu iruppavar ākiya mūvakiyiṉarum tiruntutal aritu. māṟāka varuntu taliyum uṇartaliyum pulappaṭuttukiṟavarkaḷ tiruntuvar.”

1849. “eṇṇikki kūṭutal iruntālum paṇi keṭum.”

1850. “nām ceyyat tavaṟukiṉṟa kāriyaṅkaḷāl ettakiya iḻappukkaḷ, kēṭukaḷ varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu uyttuṉarum aṟivu iruntāl ceyya vēṇṭiyaṉa etiyum ceyyātu irukkamāṭṭōm.”

1851. “tavaṟu”, ‘tappu’ eṉṟu kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum ceyyāmiyiyum collutal kūṭātu.”

1852. “uṭaliliruntu kaḻivukaḷ nīṅkuvatil ēṟpaṭum cikkal uṭalip piṇikku āḷākki viṭukiṟatu. atupōla, keṭṭap paḻakkaṅkaḷiliruntu mīḷat tayaṅkutal palavita iḻappukaḷiyum iḻivukaḷiyum koṇṭuvantu cērkkum.”

1853. “ciṟunīr, uṭal iyakkattil keṭṭup pōṉatutāṉ. atupōl keṭṭap paḻakkaṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyil tōṉṟupavitām. avi naṟpaḻakkaṅ kaḷākat tōṉṟikkūṭap piṉ kālap pōkkil keṭṭap paḻakkaṅkaḷākiyirukkalām.”

1854. “paṇattiṉ arumi iṉṟum ēḻi kaḷukkut teriyātu. kiyil aintu rūpāy koṭuttup pāruṅkaḷ.”

1855. “aṉṉi intirāviṉ āṭcip pōkkāl kaṭikkōṭiyil niṉṟa ēḻi makkaḷukku vāḻvu kiṭittatu.”

1856. “vicuvācam eṉpataṟkuriya poruḷik koccip paṭuttāmal iruntāl otta maṉattuṭaṉ pūraṇamāṉa ottuḻittaliyē kuṟikkum.”

1857. “kūṭṭuṟavu iyakka viḻāvil, cutantira tiṉa viḻāvil, kuṭiyaracut tiṉa viḻāvil ārvam kāṭṭātavarkaḷ ellām kaṭci araciyal viḻākkaḷil ārvam kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. aṟiyāmiyē kāraṇam.”

1858. “nāṭu oṉṟāka irukkavēṇṭum eṉṟu coṉṉāl pōtātu. oṉṟāka iruppataṟkuriya kāraṇaṅkaḷ vaḷarntākavēṇṭum.”

1859. “aṅkīkarikkap peṟṟa niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ colvati nampavēṇṭum. atip poy eṉṟu collalākātu. appaṭic coṉṉāl niṟuvaṉaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṭam nampikkiyi iḻakkum. araciṉ matippu kuṟiyum.”

1860. “ceyalaṟṟa nirvāka iyantiram iruppati viṭa atu illāmal iruppatē mēl.”

1861. “eḷiyavarkaḷ toṭakka niliyilēyē yātoṉṟiyum kūṟuvatilli. ataṉāl eḷitil kaḷiya muṭivatilli.”

1862. “nitiyi, varavu-celavu ceytāl maṭṭum pōtātu. niti nirvākam vēṇṭum.”

1863. “niti nirvākam poṟuppuḷḷa oṉṟu.”

1864. “nammic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa cūḻnilikku nāmum poṟuppu eṉṟu uṇartal avaciyam. ippaṭi uṇartal vēlikku untu caktiyāka utavi ceyyum.”

1865. “ovvoru kuṭumpat talivaṉum taṉatu kuḻantikaḷiṉ etirkālattip paṟṟi niṉittālē pōtum. nalla nirvākattukku ottuḻippārkaḷ.”

1866. “paṇam iruppativiṭa, periya āpattu paṇam iruppatākak kāṭṭi naṭantu koḷvatu-”

1867. “celvam uṭimiyil eḷimi, celvattiṟkup pātukāppu.”

1868. “taṉṉiṟivukku nikarāṉa valimi illi.”

1869. “coṉṉatic ceypavarkaḷ kaṭinili. aṭi oṟṟic ceypavarkaḷ mattimam. niṉippati niṉintu ceytu muṭippavarkaḷ uttamam.”

1870. “intiya nāṭṭu ēḻikaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷ mītu curaṇṭum cakti, valimiyāka uṭkārntirukkiṟatu. curaṇṭum caktiyiṭamiruntu ēḻikaḷi mīṭpatu kaṭiṉam.”

1871. “oḻuṅku muṟikaḷukku caṭṭam iyaṟṟuvataṟkākac caṭṭacapikku uṟuppiṉarkaḷit tērnteṭukkum tērtalilēyē caṭṭattiṟku mariyāti illi.”

1872. “iṉṟu caṭṭacapi uṟuppiṉar patavi nāṭṭup paṟṟukku uriyatu alla. atikāra āci, celvākkup perumi ākiyaṉavaṟṟukkēyām.”

1873. “intiyāvil tērtaliṉ viliyum uyarntuviṭṭatu.”

1874. “piḻikka māṭṭār piḻittālum vēli ceyya iyalātu eṉṟu kūṟuvataṉ mūlam etiriyiṭam evvaḷavu accam irukkiṟatu eṉpatip purintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu.”

1875. “āṭci iyantiraṅkaḷ ellām cariyākat tāṉ irukkiṉṟaṉa. iyaṅkuvatutāṉ illi.”

1876. “poṟuppuḷḷa vāḻkki eṉpatu puṉita māṉatu. aṉpi āḻappaṭuttum; naṭpi uṟutip paṭuttum; nalattik kūṭṭum; iṉpatti vaḷarkkum, aḻivit taṭuttu niṟuttum.”

1877. “enta vakiyāṉa piriviṉi eṇṇaṅkaḷum kuḻantikaḷ maṉattil patiyāvaṇṇam pārttuk koḷḷal avaciyam.”

1878. “namatu talimuṟiyil nām kaṇṭa māperuntalivarkaḷiyaṭuttu vanta talimuṟiyiṉar talivarkaḷ peyari maṭṭumē uccarikkiṉṟaṉar. talivarkaḷuṭiya koḷkikaḷi viṭṭu viṭṭaṉar.”

talivarkaḷ peyarip payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟkuriya kāraṇam attalivarkaḷ makkaḷāl aṅkīkarikkap peṟṟavarkaḷ. attalivarkaḷukku makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷa aṅkīkāra niḻalilēyē vāḻa ācippaṭukiṉṟaṉar.”

1879. “makkaḷ kūṭit toḻil ceyyum poḻutellām oṉṟiraṇṭu kāvalkārarkaḷ anta iṭattil iruppatu tavirkka muṭiyāta tēviyāki viṭṭatu.”

1880. “cāta”i uṇarcci carācari maṉitaṉiṭam kūṭa iṭam peṟṟu varukiṟatu.”

1881. “enta oru paṇikkum tiṭṭamiṭutal tavirkka iyalāta tēvi.”

1882. “uḻippiṉ aṭippaṭiyil ūtiyam eṉṟāl pala aluvalarkaḷ paṭṭiṉi kiṭakka vēṇṭi varum.”

1883. “uḻittal, uḻalutal, eṉṟa coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻiyil piṟantatu “uḻippu” eṉṟa col. atāvatu kaṭumiyāṉa cōtaṉikaḷit tāṅki oru paṇiyi muṉippuṭaṉ ceytu payaṉ tarattakka vakiyil muṭittal uḻippu ākum.”

1884. “tērtalil yār veṟṟi peṟuvār eṉpatu vākkukaḷ eṇṇi aṟivittap piṟaku, kēṭkappeṟum ceytiyi vittē muṭivu ceyyavēṇṭum. atu variyil kēṭpatu ellām avaravarkaḷuṭiya āciyiyēyām.”

1885. “kirāmappuṟaṅkaḷil puñciyil pātikku mēl taricu. ataṉālēyē kiḻakku irāmanātapuram vaṟumi niṟinta piratēcamākak kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu,”

1886. “uḷḷīṭu illāta maṉitarkaḷ muraṭṭut taṉamāka naṭantukoḷvārkaḷ. āṉāl vañcaṉi irukkātu. uḷḷiṭu uḷḷavarkaḷil palar nākarikamāka naṭantukoḷvar. āṉālum vañcaṉi irukkum.”

1887. “vāḻkkiyil uṉṉuṭaṉ ētāvatoru nōkkattil uṭaṉuḻippavarkaḷākap paṇiyāḷarkaḷ amintāl nallatu. vēliyi, vēlikkārar eṉṟa maṉappāṉmiyil pārppatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa oru vitamāṉa vēcittaṉamākum.”

1888. “iṉṉamum kirāmappuṟaṅkaḷil peṇ kuḻantikaḷ palar paḷḷikkuc cellavilli.”

1889. “puraṭcit talivar matuvilakkimaṭṭum rattu ceyyāmal iruntiruntāl makkaḷiṭam mikap periya aṉpip peṟṟiruppār.”

1890. “ottuvarāta ceytikaḷil toṭakkattilēyē kaṇṭippu illātiruttal tavaṟu. piṉ viḷivukaḷ mōcamākiviṭum.”

1891. “ceytikaḷ, uṇmik kalappuṭiyaṉa. vatantikaḷ.poymiyum puṉinturiyum kalantavi.”

1892. “amerikkā otuṅki viṭṭālē ilaṅkic cikkal tāṉē tīrntuviṭum.”

1893. “vilaṅkukaḷukkuḷḷa uṟavuṇarvu, maṉitarkaḷiṭam illi.”

1894. “vēli ceypavarkaḷ ellām uḻippāḷikaḷ allar.”

1895. “palaṉik kaṇakkiṭṭuttāṉ uḻippiṉ matippik kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyum.”

1896. “intiya vākkaraḷarkaḷiṭam “akaṭa vikaṭaṅkaḷ” cellu paṭiyākavilli,”

1897. “rājīv kāntiyiṉ pēccu, paḻiya nāṭṭut talivarkaḷi niṉiviṟkuk koṇṭuvarukiṟatu.”

1898. “oru mātamāka iravu pakalāka uḻittataṟku tamiḻ nāṭṭu makkaḷ koṭutta paricu” itutāṉ eṉṟāl tamiḻ makkaḷukkākavā uḻittārkaḷ? taṅkaḷ kaṭcip patavikku varattāṉē uḻittārkaḷ. kaṭcikku uḻippataṟkup paricu makkaḷ eppaṭi taruvārkaḷ?”

1899. “jaṉatāvil cila uyarnta marapukaḷuṭiya periya araciyal mētikaḷ uḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷum pārāḷumaṉṟattiṟku varātatu ēmāṟṟattit tarukiṟatu. piratamar rājiv ivarkaḷi rājya capikku vara ēṟpāṭu ceytal nallatu.”

1900. “namatu nāṭṭu makkaḷukkup paṇattil uḷḷa āciyi, poruḷkaḷ mītāka maṭi māṟṟa vēṇṭum.”

1901. “kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ vaḷam muṭaṅkik kiṭakkiṟatu.”

1902. araciyal veṟiyum nallatalla.

1903. “namakku vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ namatu vaḷarccikkut tuṇi ceyya vēṇṭumē tavira calukikaḷ tarakkūṭātu.”

1904. “mitamāka uṇṭāl, kaṭumiyāka uḻittāl, āṉmā camaniliyil iruntāl, etaṟkum payappaṭa vēṇṭiyatilli.”

1905. “nām nākarikamāka naṭantu koḷvatāl tōṟkalām. āṉāl kollippuṟa veṟṟikaḷ tōlviyiyē tarum.”





inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
